# EuroPrint ERP — 180-PUNKT SAP-AUDIT · YAKUNIY ROLL-UP

> Sana: 2026-06-06 · Rol: 🔵 BIRLASHTIRUVCHI (QAT'IY READ-ONLY) · Metod: VERIFY-DON'T-TRUST
> Manba: 60 agent × 12 paket-fayl (`docs/audit/parts/001..166.md`) + mustaqil jonli kod/DB qayta-tekshiruv (`_audit/q.cjs`).
> Jonli DB = `europrint` (qurilish bosqichi — operatsion jadvallar deyarli bo'sh; "0 qator" = oqim jonli hech qachon ishlamagan = dalil).

> **Birlashtiruvchi izohi:** men 10+ asosiy hukmni kod va DB bilan mustaqil qayta-tekshirdim — **hammasi tasdiqlandi**.
> Faqat bitta nuans qo'shildi (pastda): CC `MANAGER_OF_SENDER` — org-daraxt fallback'i BOR (lekin `head_user_id` 124/142 NULL bo'lgani uchun deyarli har doim baribir tushadi).

## 6. ⭐ GOLDEN-THREAD UZILISH NUQTASI

**Buyurtma → I.Chiqarish → Ombor → Moliya zanjiri BIRINCHI bo'g'inda uziladi:**

```
CRM lead→deal  ──►  SD order INSERT  ──X──►  Production  ──►  MES  ──►  QC  ──►  Ombor  ──►  Yetkazish  ──►  Moliya/GL
  (kod ulangan,      (sales_orders=12,    ⛔ UZILGAN        (no-op   (qc_insp  (stocks≠  (orderId      (entries=0,
   crm_deals=0)       REAL INSERT)        FK=0 +            stub)    =0)       warehouse  undefined     qo'lda only)
                                          listener YO'Q)                       _stock)    →skip)
```

**Aniq uzilish:** `sales_orders` (12 qator, REAL) dan keyin **SD→Production ko'prig'i yo'q**:
1. `orders` jadval 2026-06-06 DROP qilingan (`to_regclass('orders')`=NULL) → ikki-buyurtma-dunyo "soddalashdi" lekin ko'prik ham yo'qoldi.
2. `pp.module.ts` listenerlar orasida `OrderCreatedEvent` YO'Q (faqat 5: Advance/MroStop/Design/LabTest/WmsGoodsIssued).
3. Natija: undan keyingi hamma bo'g'in (MES no-op, QC=0, QC→Ombor noto'g'ri `stocks` jadval, Ombor→Yetkazish orderId=undefined→skip) **jonli hech qachon ishlamaydi**.

**Yagona REAL modullararo bog'lanish = avans→5 bo'lim fan-out** (`advance-approved-fanout.listener.ts`, lekin `sd_order_departments`=0 — kod real, jonli tsetiklanmagan).

**10 zanjir bo'g'inidan ~1 ishlaydi. SAP-darajasi: 🔴.**

## 7. ⭐ BUGUN FOYDALANUVCHI NIMA REAL QILA OLADI (halol javob)

### ✅ REAL ishlaydi (bugun, jonli):
- **Login + RBAC** — 4 global guard, server-enforce, `position_permissions`=1380, `users`=31.
- **SD ko'rish** — `sales_orders`=12, `sd_customers`=9 ko'rinadi (VIEW orqali).
- **Ombor skan (kirim/chiqim)** — POS Monitor atomik stok o'zgartiradi (`warehouse_stock`=25, FEFO/balance-guard).
- **Material ko'rish** — `material_cards`=21 Excel-grid.
- **Kunlik hisobot** — `daily_reports`=**3150 jonli qator** (eng to'la operatsion jadval, audit bilan).
- **Org-sxema + Portret** — `org_departments`=142 interaktiv tree, export.
- **Chat** — jonli xonalar/xabarlar, WS.
- **Audit** — `audit_logs`=9342 (haqiqatan ishlaydigan).
- **Kanban / LMS / avans hisoblash** — CRUD real.

### 🔴 BUGUN YO'Q (yoki yolg'on):
- **Pul/kassa** — `cash_sessions`=0, `retail_pos_transactions`=0, kassir-hub yo'q.
- **GL/moliya yozuvi** — `entries`=0, `gl_entries`=0 (pul oqimi orol).
- **Ishlab chiqarish rejasi** — `work_centers`=0, routing-create stub, MES sessiya 0.
- **Oylik to'lov** — shartnoma/payroll_calc=0; payroll→GL UZUQ.
- **Uchma-uch buyurtma** — SD→Production uzilgan, hech qachon zavodga tushmaydi.
- **CC hujjat oqimi** — `manager_id` 30/30 NULL → 1-inboxga yetmaydi.

**Bir gapda:** Foydalanuvchi bugun **kira oladi, ko'ra oladi, ombor skanlay oladi, material va kunlik hisobot bilan ishlay oladi** — lekin **pul, ishlab chiqarish rejasi, oylik, GL, uchma-uch buyurtma oqimi YO'Q**. Bu **demo emas — to'ldirilmagan/ulanmagan real** (kod bor, data va ko'prik yo'q).

## 8. SAP BO'LISH UCHUN NIMA KERAK (TOP yetishmaydigan — tartib bilan)

1. **O'zak (P0):**
   - `employees.manager_id` backfill (org FK'dan) → CC/eskalatsiya tiriladi.
   - Kanonik order jadval tanlash (`sales_orders`) + `OrderCreatedEvent` listener → SD→Production ko'prik.
   - GL avto-posting listener (har iqtisodiy hodisa → `entries`).
2. **Bitta oltin ip:** Buyurtma→Avans→I.Chiq→Ombor→Yetkazish→Moliya — boshdan-oxir bitta real buyurtma jonli o'tkazish.
3. **Master-data birlashtirish:** 2 order / 7 GL / 2 stock / 9 attendance / 2 lead → bitta haqiqat manbai (SAP Universal Journal tamoyili).
4. **Stub-to'ldirish:** kg↔list/gofra/kashirovka formula, sex-tur taksonomiyasi, routing-create, MES→QC, IoT-tablet, SMM-AI.
5. **Master data: 952 jadvalda 193 FK (20%)** → referential integrity; `head_user_id` 124/142 NULL to'ldirish.
6. **Data + test:** tor bir bo'lakda jonli ishga tushirish + vizyon-modul spec'lar + real DB integ (testcontainer) + Semgrep config.
7. **i18n/UI:** sidebar `titleKey` migratsiya, `format.ts` locale parametrlash, a11y (aria/WCAG), DataTableRedesign'ni 175 jadvalga ulash.

# 180-PUNKT SAP-AUDIT — Bo'lak 001 (punktlar 001–015)

> Qat'iy READ-ONLY tahlil. Manba: jonli kod (`apps/api/src/modules/`), jonli DB (`_audit/q.cjs`, `europrint` bo'sh-qurilish DB), FE (`artifacts/erp-dashboard/src/pages/`).
> Eslatma: DB deyarli BO'SH (qurilish bosqichi) — "0 qator" demak yozuvchi yo'q degani EMAS; har jadval uchun yozuvchi-kod alohida tekshirildi.

---

## [001] CRM/Savdo core

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Leads list/get/create/stage/qualify | crm-leads.controller.ts:100–203 → leads.service.ts → ICrmLeadsRepository | crm_leads 0 (yozuvchi bor: repo.create/update/softDelete) | CRMWorkspace.tsx, CRMActivities.tsx | crm-leads-ops.dto.spec (faqat Zod) | REAL — lead boshqaruvi ishlaydi | REAL |
| Lead email log | crm-leads.controller.ts:185 → leadsService.logEmail | crm_activities 0 (yozuvchi: logEmail) | CRMWorkspace | yo'q | REAL — pochta provayderi yo'q, halol "queued" activity yoziladi (avval green-lie edi, tuzatilgan) | REAL |
| Deals list/get/create/won/stage/patch/delete | crm-deals.controller.ts:79–203 → CommandBus (Create/MarkWon/UpdateStage/Update/Delete) + DealsService.findAll/create | deals 0 (yozuvchi: CQRS handlerlar) | CRMWorkspace | yo'q (integ.) | REAL — bitim hayot-sikli to'liq | REAL |
| Quick deal | crm-deals.controller.ts:191 → dealsService.create | deals 0 | CRMWorkspace QuickCreate | yo'q | REAL | REAL |
| Companies / contacts / activities / pipelines / custom-fields | crm-companies/contacts/activities/pipelines/custom-fields.controller.ts | crm_companies/contacts/activities/pipelines/custom_fields = 0 (yozuvchi controllerlar) | CRMWorkspace, CRMSettings | dto.spec lar | REAL (CRUD jadvalga bog'langan) | REAL |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP CRM / Odoo CRM: lead→opportunity→quotation→order pipeline, faollik tarixi, scoring. **Bizda:** lead/deal/company/contact/activity/pipeline CRUD jonli, CQRS + Result pattern, Roles-guard. **Gap:** lead↔deal↔SD-order ko'prik kodi to'la avtomatlashmagan (qo'lda convert); pipeline-stage konfiguratsiyasi cheklangan; aktiv 0 qator = real foydalanish hali yo'q. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim** (arxitektura SAP-darajasi, lekin integratsiya zanjiri va jonli ma'lumot yo'q).

### Muammolar
- KOD↔DB mos: yozuvchilar bor, lekin 5 jadval ham 0 qator — modul qurilgan, ishlatilmagan (FOYDA potensial, jonli emas).
- TEST gap: faqat DTO (Zod) spec lar — controller/service integ-test YO'Q (5-tomonning TEST tomoni zaif).
- `getById`/`getLeadEmails` `findOne` natijasini ataylab `unwrapOrThrow`'siz qaytaradi (izoh bilan to'g'ri) — lekin shakl nomuvofiqligi xavfi bor.

### 3-qator xulosa
CRM core to'liq REAL: lead/deal/company/contact CRUD jonli DB'ga yozadi, CQRS+Result+Roles bilan SAP-uslub arxitektura.
Asosiy kamchilik — integ-test yo'qligi va barcha jadvallar bo'sh (qurilgan, ishlatilmagan), hamda lead→deal→order avtomatik ko'prik to'la emas.
Foydaga tayyor, lekin jonli ma'lumotsiz va zanjir uzilgan.

### SAP-taqqos
SAP Sales Cloud / Einstein: ML-asosli lead scoring, churn prediction real signal'lardan. **Bizda IKKI dunyo:** (a) `CrmAiService` (ai.controller) — REAL: DB o'qiydi, deterministik scoreLead/forecastDeal/NBA hisoblaydi, lead_score yozadi (SAP-uslub, ML emas lekin halol rule-based). (b) `CrmAiExtendedService` (ai-extended controller, `/crm/ai/*`) — **butunlay FAKE**: hardcoded `'Manufacturing'`, `score:65`, `churn:medium`. **Gap:** ai-extended dunyosi soxta; `createNbaTask` green-lie (crm_tasks bor lekin yozilmaydi). **SAP-darajasi: 🔴 yo'q** (ai-extended); halol rule-based qism 🟡.

### Muammolar (dalil bilan)
- **GREEN-LIE (eng jiddiy):** `crm-ai-extended.controller.ts:140` — `{ created: true, taskId: Date.now() }`. `crm_tasks` jadvali MAVJUD (DB tasdiq), lekin INSERT yo'q. FE `AIAnalysisPanel.tsx` buni chaqiradi — foydalanuvchi vazifa yaratildi deb o'ylaydi, real yo'q. Bu Q-40/Qoida 10 buzilishi.
- **FAKE-DATA (7 metod):** butun `CrmAiExtendedService` DB'ga umuman bormaydi — har so'rovga aynan bir xil qotirilgan javob (industry/segment/budget/score/churn).
- **Ikki AI servis chalkashligi:** `CrmAiService` (REAL) ╳ `CrmAiExtendedService` (FAKE) — ikkalasi ham `/crm/ai/*` ostida, dublikat yo'nalish riski (`/crm/ai/nba/:entityType/:entityId` ikkala controllerda bor).
- TEST: faqat DTO spec — soxta-javoblarni ushlovchi test yo'q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Punkt nomi to'g'ri: `createNbaTask` aynan green-lie (timestamp-id, DB yozmaydi) — `crm_tasks` jadvali bo'sh qoladi.
Butun `CrmAiExtendedService` (autofill/churn/score/leads/nba) FAKE-DATA — hardcoded javoblar, foydasi nol.
Yagona haqiqiy qism — alohida `CrmAiService` (DB-asosli rule-based scoring); ikki servisni birlashtirib soxtalarini olib tashlash kerak.

## [003] SD orders (sales_orders/sd_orders kanonik?)

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| List/get/create/status orders | sd-orders.controller.ts:79–213 → CommandBus/QueryBus (Create/UpdateStatus/...) | sales_orders 0 (yozuvchi: drizzle-sd-orders.repo.ts) | Sales/Orders FE | yo'q | REAL — buyurtma hayot-sikli | REAL |
| Advance payment + bypass + tech-checkpoint | sd-orders.controller.ts:163–200 → ConfirmAdvancePayment/ApproveAdvanceBypass/ApproveTechCheckpoint | sales_orders + sd_advance_idempotency_keys (yozuvchi bor) | Finance/SD FE | yo'q | REAL — avans 70% darvozasi (owner asosiy vizyon) | REAL |
| CSV export | sd-orders.controller.ts:51-75 | sales_orders o'qiydi | SD FE | yo'q | REAL | REAL |
| Order repo writes | drizzle-sd-orders.repo.ts:30–123 → `db.select/update().from(salesOrders)` + `sales_orders` soft-delete | **sales_orders = BASE (kanonik)** | — | yo'q | REAL | REAL |

### Kanoniklik (asosiy savol)
DB tasdiq: `sales_orders` = **BASE TABLE** (yozuvchi: drizzle-sd-orders.repo); `sd_sales_orders` = **VIEW** (sales_orders ustidan, alohida dunyo EMAS). Repo barcha yozuvni `salesOrders`/`sales_orders`'ga qiladi (repo.ts:30,81,88,96,104,115). **Xulosa: KANONIK = `sales_orders`**; `sd_orders` nomli jadval umuman yo'q (faqat nomli modul papkasi). Memory tasdig'iga mos.

### SAP-taqqos
SAP SD (VA01/VA02): sales order + item + schedule line + credit check + ATP. **Bizda:** order header CRUD + avans-darvoza + tech-checkpoint + dept fan-out (owner vizyoni) REAL; item'lar `sales_order_items` (0, yozuvchi bor). **Gap:** kredit-limit/ATP/availability check yo'q; ikki order-dunyo (`sales_orders` ╳ `orders` BASE) hali to'la yarashmagan (`orders` jadvali 2026-06-06 DROP qilingan — yaxshilangan). **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim**.

### Muammolar
- Kanoniklik HAL: `sales_orders` BASE, `sd_sales_orders` VIEW — eski "ikki dunyo" tashvishi SD ichida yo'q (faqat `orders` BASE bilan PP tomonida edi, u DROP bo'lgan).
- TEST YO'Q: SD orders'da integ-test yo'q — avans/checkpoint kabi muhim moliyaviy oqim testsiz.
- Barcha jadval 0 qator — jonli buyurtma yo'q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Kanonik javob aniq: `sales_orders` = BASE (yagona haqiqat), `sd_sales_orders` = VIEW, `sd_orders` yo'q.
Order CRUD + avans-darvoza + tech-checkpoint to'liq REAL, owner asosiy vizyoniga (70% avans→dept fan-out) xizmat qiladi.
Kamchilik — kredit/ATP yo'q, integ-test yo'q, jonli data yo'q.

SAP SD: customer master (XD01), quotation (VA21)→order. Odoo: customer + quotation→sale order. **Bizda:** mijoz-master to'liq (360, contacts, NPS, complaints, competitors, internal-intelligence layer); quotation→order convert REAL; narx-formula singleton. **Gap:** `customers` jadvali yo'q — AI/ba'zi modul `customers` kutadi, SD `sd_customers` ishlatadi (bo'linish riski, memory'da qayd); kredit-limit yo'q. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim** (mijoz-master SAP-darajasiga yaqin, lekin master-data bo'linishi va jonli data yo'q).

Customers (360 ko'rinish, kontakt/NPS/shikoyat) va quotations (convert-to-order, narx-formula) to'liq REAL, service+repo qatlamli.

Master-data bo'linishi (`sd_customers` ╳ kutilgan `customers`) va integ-test yo'qligi asosiy kamchilik.

**Eng kuchli:** [013] SPC/DPMO — haqiqiy SAP-grade matematika (Shewhart, Cp/Cpk, sigma-level). [011]/[012] QC, [001] CRM core, [003]/[004] SD — mustahkam REAL CQRS+Result arxitektura.

**Eng zaif:** [002] CRM AI-extended — `createNbaTask` green-lie + butun servis FAKE-DATA. [010] Design AI — `Math.random()` verifyDesign + soxta mockup URL. [015] Technology — markaziy generateCard/optimizeCard 501 + material-alternativ FAKE.

**Asosiy naqshlar:** (1) Core CRUD ham har joyda REAL; (2) "AI" yuzalar deyarli har modulda soxta yoki 501 (CRM-extended, design-AI, tech-gen); (3) 501 lar asosan HALOL belgilangan (Qoida 17/Q-40 hurmati), lekin bir nechta haqiqiy green-lie/fake qoldi; (4) DB deyarli bo'sh — yozuvchilar bor, jonli data yo'q; (5) integ-test deyarli yo'q (faqat DTO Zod + FE smoke), SPC formulalari ham testsiz.

### SAP-taqqos
SAP OEE = Availability×Performance×Quality (real timeticket'dan). Bizda OEE `production_sessions.oee` ustunini AVG qiladi — ya'ni OEE oldindan hisoblangan ustun, A×P×Q dekompozitsiyasi yo'q. **OEE manba jadvali drift:** OEE `production_sessions`dan o'qiydi, lekin sessiyalar `mes_sessions`/`mes_production_sessions`ga yoziladi → OEE hech qachon yangi sessiyani ko'rmaydi. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim.**

2. SAP gap: OEE = oldindan-hisoblangan ustun AVG, real Availability/Performance/Quality dekompozitsiyasi yo'q.

| `PosGlAutoListener` (event) | `pos-gl-auto.listener.ts:34` (`@EventsHandler PosMovementCompletedEvent`) | — | — | yo'q | ⭐ DEAD-LETTER — hech kim event publish qilmaydi | ORPHAN |

GL aslida inline qilingan (`pos-movement-status.service.ts:203`).
2. KOD: `postToLedger` NON-FATAL — `gl_account_mappings` bo'lmasa jim o'tadi (warning) → harakat tasdiqlanadi-yu, `entries`ga hech narsa tushmaydi (mapping=6 bor, lekin to'liqligi tekshirilmagan).
3. TEST: 0 ta. `entries`=0, `pos_gl_postings`=0 — hali hech qaysi harakat tasdiqlanmagan.

POS GL endi kanonik `entries`ga REAL post qiladi (no-op TUZATILGAN), inline 5-bosqich log AWAITING_REVIEW bilan. Lekin ikki event-listener O'LIK dead-letter (publisher yo'q). Ledger-post non-fatal (mapping bo'lmasa jim).

Bizda **3+ GL olam**: (1) `entries` — payment/POS GL bu yerga REAL post qiladi (kanonik); (2) `gl_documents` — manual `postDocument` faqat HEADER yozadi (satr yo'q!), trial-balance buni KO'RMAYDI; (3) `gl_journal_entries`+`gl_lines` — ishlatilmaydigan o'lik model. **SAP-darajasi: 🔴 yo'q (3-olam, post↔read uzilgan).**

### Muammolar
1. KOD↔DB GREEN-LIE: `postDocument` (`repo:44`) `glDocuments`ga FAQAT header yozadi (lines yo'q), `getTrialBalance` (`repo:96`) `entries`dan o'qiydi → manual GL hujjat to'lov balansiga HECH QACHON ta'sir qilmaydi. Post 200 qaytaradi, lekin trial-balance o'zgarmaydi.
2. KOD↔KOD: payment GL `gl-posting.service.ts` → kanonik `entries` (to'g'ri olam), lekin `finance/gl postDocument` → `gl_documents` (boshqa olam) — ikki yozuvchi ikki jadvalga.
3. DATA: `accounts`=42 (COA REAL JONLI) — yagona JONLI GL ma'lumot; barcha jurnal jadvallari 0.

### 3-qator xulosa
GL 3-olam: payment-GL kanonik `entries`ga REAL post (to'g'ri), lekin manual `postDocument` `gl_documents`ga faqat header yozadi va trial-balance `entries`dan o'qigani uchun KO'RINMAYDI (GREEN-LIE). COA=42 JONLI, jurnallar 0. Post↔read uzilgan.

## [029] Finance payments

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST `finance/payments/record` | `finance-payments.controller.ts:100` → `record-payment.handler.ts:39` (`recordPayment`+`postCustomerPayment`→`entries`) | `payments`=0, `entries`=0 | FinanceApproval.tsx | yo'q | ⭐ REAL — invoice topish, overpay-guard, cents-arifmetika, partial/full, GL provodka, domain event | REAL |
| POST `finance/payments` (root) | `finance-payments.controller.ts:73` | — | Finance | yo'q | `throw NotImplementedException` (record ishlatish kerak) | 501-STUB |
| POST `:paymentId/verify` | `finance-payments.controller.ts:123` | — | Finance | yo'q | `return { message:'Payment verified' }` — DB'ga yozmaydi | GREEN-LIE |
| GET `:invoiceId/outstanding` | `finance-payments.controller.ts:134` | — | Finance | yo'q | `return { outstanding: 0 }` hardcoded | FAKE-DATA |
| PATCH/POST `:id/approve` | `finance-payments.controller.ts:86,143` → `FinanceActionsService.approvePayment` | `payments`=0 | Finance | yo'q | REAL approve | REAL |

- **DB:** `payments`=0, `invoices`/`finance_invoices`=0, `entries`=0 (qurilish bosqichi).

### SAP-taqqos
SAP FI-AR = F-28 incoming payment + clearing + partial/residual. Bizda `record` yo'li SAP-darajasi: invoice lookup, overpayment guard, cents-precision (NaN-himoya), partial/full status, kanonik `entries`ga GL provodka (Dr Cash/Cr AR), domain event. Lekin `verify` GREEN-LIE va `outstanding` FAKE. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim (record REAL, verify/outstanding soxta).**

### Muammolar
1. KOD GREEN-LIE: `verifyPayment` (`:123`) `{ message:'Payment verified', paymentId }` qaytaradi — HECH QANDAY DB UPDATE yo'q. "Tasdiqlandi" deydi, lekin holat o'zgarmaydi (Q-40/Q-43 buzilishi).
2. KOD FAKE-DATA: `getOutstandingPayment` (`:134`) `{ outstanding: 0 }` hardcoded — invoice qoldig'ini hisoblamaydi, doim 0.
3. TEST: 0 ta.

### 3-qator xulosa
`record` yo'li SAP-darajasi REAL (overpay-guard, cents, partial/full, kanonik `entries` GL, event). Root POST=501 (to'g'ri). Lekin `verify`=GREEN-LIE (yozmaydi) va `outstanding`=FAKE (doim 0).

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET/POST `finance/budgets` | `budgets.service.ts:38` → `drizzle-finance-budgets.repo.ts:62` (`db.insert(budgets)`) | `budgets`=0 | BudgetManagement.tsx | yo'q | REAL CRUD budgets | REAL |
| PATCH update / approve | `budgets.service.ts:46,55` (approve: draft-gate) | `budgets`=0 | BudgetManagement | yo'q | REAL — faqat draft tasdiqlanadi (status-gate) | REAL |
| DELETE (soft) | `budgets.service.ts:66` → `softDelete` | `budgets`=0 | BudgetManagement | yo'q | REAL soft-delete | REAL |
| Budget lines | `budgets.service.ts:85` → `repo:110` (`db.insert(budget_lines)`) | `budget_lines`=0 | BudgetManagement | yo'q | REAL satr CRUD | REAL |

- **DB:** `budgets`=0, `budget_lines`=0 (qurilish bosqichi).

### SAP-taqqos
SAP CO budget = KP06/KPZ2 + availability control (AVAC). Bizda budget header+lines CRUD + approve workflow (draft→approved status-gate). Yaxshi asos. Yetishmaydi: availability control (xarajatni budjet bilan tekshirish), commitment, fund reservation. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim (CRUD+approve REAL, AVAC yo'q).**

2. FE↔BE: BudgetManagement.tsx smoke-test bor, lekin unit/integration test yo'q.
3. TEST: 0 ta backend spec.

### 3-qator xulosa
Budgets REAL — header+lines CRUD, approve status-gate (faqat draft), soft-delete, hammasi `budgets`/`budget_lines`ga haqiqiy yoziladi. SAP availability-control yo'q (passiv budjet). Jadval bo'sh.

## [032] Finance tax/salary

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GeneralTaxService (VAT INCLUSIVE/EXCLUSIVE) | finance/application/general-tax.service.ts:75-153 (tax_rate_config'dan o'qiydi) | **tax_rate_config jadval YO'Q** (✗ exists) | — (HTTP route yo'q) | — | runtime'da loadActiveRate CRASH | **ORPHAN** (route yo'q + jadval yo'q) |
| fi/tax/general-tax.service.ts | fi/tax/general-tax.service.ts:5 (re-export shim) | — | — | — | shim | DUPLICATE (shim) |
| POST /payroll/calculate-tax (INPS8+JSHD12) | payroll-periods.controller.ts:64-74 (hardcoded TAX_RATE=12, PENSION=8, controller ichida hisob) | DB yozmaydi | PayrollAutomation/FinanceDashboardPayrollTab | smoke (Payroll) | qaytaradi lekin saqlamaydi | **GREEN-LIE** (echo-calc) |
| GET /payroll/tax-summary | finance-payroll.controller.ts:43 → service→repo.taxSummary | payroll_* = 0 | FinanceDashboardPayrollTab | — | bo'sh | REAL (bo'sh) |
| GET /finance/payroll/tax-calendar | finance-extended-payroll.controller.ts:96 | — | — | — | ko'rilmadi (deferred) | (tekshirilmadi) |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP'da soliq = tax procedure (TAXUZ) condition-based, VAT/INPS/JSHD avtomatik posting bilan; payroll soliqlari PY moduli. Bizda IKKI ZID FALSAFA: (a) GeneralTaxService VAT'ni DB-config'dan to'g'ri hisoblaydi — lekin `tax_rate_config` jadval yo'q + hech qaysi controller chaqirmaydi (o'lik); (b) `calculate-tax` INPS/JSHD'ni controller ichida hardcode qiladi (Qoida 12 buzilishi) va saqlamaydi; (c) qolgan payroll esa "gross-only, soliq 1C'da" deydi (finance-extended-payroll.service.ts:18). Uchta nuqtai nazar bir-biriga zid. SAP-darajasi past — soliq yagona, izchil, persistent emas.

### Muammolar
1. `tax_rate_config` jadval DB'da YO'Q (`q.cjs` → "отношение не существует") → GeneralTaxService.loadActiveRate ishga tushsa CRASH; lekin uni hech kim chaqirmaydi → ORPHAN.
2. `calculate-tax` (payroll-periods.controller.ts:68-69) magic number 12/8, controller ichida hisob (Qoida 6+12), DB'ga yozilmaydi → GREEN-LIE.
3. "gross-only (1C)" ╳ "calculate-tax (12%/8%)" ╳ "GeneralTaxService (config)" = 3 ta nomuvofiq soliq yondashuvi; FE qaysi biriga ishonadi — noaniq.

### 3-qator xulosa
Soliq mantig'i parchalangan: GeneralTaxService o'lik (jadvalsiz, routsiz), calculate-tax hardcoded echo (saqlamaydi), HR/payroll esa gross-only deydi.
Hech bir yo'l soliqni real DB'ga persist qilmaydi.
Verdikt: tax ko'p qismi ORPHAN/GREEN-LIE; professional ERP soliq moduli darajasidan uzoq.

## [033] Finance payroll-posting (balans)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PayrollClosureService.buildJournal (balansli debit/credit) | hr/payroll/payroll-closure.service.ts:117-141 (sof domain, balans tekshiruvi >0.5) | — | — | spec ehtimol bor | balansli jurnal quradi | REAL (domain) |
| closePeriod → insertGlJournalLines | hr/payroll/payroll.service.ts:55-106 + drizzle-hr-payroll.repo.ts:87-115 | gl_journal_entries=0 | FinanceDashboardPayrollTab | — | yozadi LEKIN buzuq | **GREEN-LIE (semantik)** |
| GL insert: debit==credit account | drizzle-hr-payroll.repo.ts:101-103 (`debit_account: line.account, credit_account: line.account`) | — | — | — | qo'sh-yozuv buziladi | **BUG** |
| POST /hr/payroll/:id/post-to-gl | hr-payroll.controller.ts:133-142 (faqat status='paid', GL yozmaydi) | — | — | — | nom yolg'on | **GREEN-LIE** |
| FinanceExtendedPayroll.calculate (real INSERT) | finance-extended-payroll.service.ts:177-204 (payroll_calculations INSERT .returning) | payroll_calculations=0 | CalculatePayrollDialog | — | real saqlaydi | REAL (bo'sh) |
| runPayroll (batch, idempotent) | finance-extended-payroll.service.ts:369-417 | payroll_contracts=0 | PayrollAutomation | smoke | real, idempotent | REAL (bo'sh) |
| finance/payroll calculatePeriod | finance/payroll/payroll.service.ts:53-78 (in-memory hisob, faqat status update — rows persist YO'Q) | — | FinanceDashboardPayrollTab | — | qator-calc saqlanmaydi | **GREEN-LIE (qisman)** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP PY'da posting run → balansli FI hujjat (Dr xarajat / Cr majburiyat) gl_account'larga, qat'iy debit=credit. Bizda buildJournal balansni TO'G'RI quradi (sof domain), lekin repo uni `gl_journal_entries`ga yozayotganda HAR satr uchun `debit_account == credit_account` qo'yadi (drizzle-hr-payroll.repo.ts:101-103) → qo'sh-yozuv yo'qoladi, har satr o'z-o'ziga Dr/Cr. Bundan tashqari `gl_journal_entries` = UNUSED GL model (canon = `entries`/`gl_entries` per memory) → posting noto'g'ri jadvalga tushadi. `post-to-gl` endpoint umuman GL yozmaydi (faqat status). SAP-darajasi: domain balans mantig'i bor, lekin persistence buzuq + noto'g'ri ledger → real double-entry EMAS.

### Muammolar
1. **drizzle-hr-payroll.repo.ts:101-103** — `debit_account: line.account, credit_account: line.account` har satr bir xil hisob ikki tomonda → buildJournal'ning balansli Dr/Cr semantikasi YO'Q bo'ladi (dalil: kod).
2. `gl_journal_entries` ga yozadi (=0); memory bo'yicha canon GL = `entries`/`gl_entries` → posting parallel/o'lik modelga tushadi (ikki-GL-dunyo).
3. `POST /hr/payroll/:id/post-to-gl` (hr-payroll.controller.ts:133) faqat `status='paid'` — GL jurnal YOZMAYDI, ammo nomi "post-to-gl" → GREEN-LIE.
4. finance/payroll `calculatePeriod` (payroll.service.ts:58-66) net'ni in-memory hisoblaydi, payroll_rows'ga qayta yozmaydi → keyingi o'qishda calc yo'qoladi.

### 3-qator xulosa
Payroll-balans domain mantig'i (buildJournal) to'g'ri va balansli, lekin DB-persist bosqichida debit==credit qilib yoziladi → qo'sh-yozuv buziladi va unused GL jadvaliga tushadi.
post-to-gl endpoint nomiga qarshi GL yozmaydi (GREEN-LIE); calculate/runPayroll esa REAL (lekin bo'sh).
Verdikt: posting "yashil lekin noto'g'ri" — Q-40 ning klassik holati.

## UMUMIY XULOSA (031–045)

**Eng kuchli (SAP-ga yaqin):** [035] daily-report (3150 jonli qator, audit trail), [041] offboarding (to'liq workflow + 3 case), [042] advances (sozlanadigan limit + idempotency), [034] employees (to'liq CRUD).

**Eng zaif:** [032] tax (GeneralTaxService orphan + jadval yo'q + calculate-tax hardcoded echo), [033] payroll-posting (debit==credit bug + unused GL jadval), [031] loans (501 + jadvalsiz).

**Takrorlanuvchi pattern (verify-don't-trust tasdiqlari):**
- "REAL lekin bo'sh" — aksariyat HR/LMS jadvallari 0 qator (qurilish bosqichi).
- GREEN-LIE (semantik) — payroll post-to-gl (status-only), GL debit==credit, market-analysis POST, enps respond surveyId:0, calculate-tax echo, hr-dashboard documents/*.
- DUPLICATE/ORPHAN jadval — gl_journal_entries╳entries, lms_*╳courses/certificates/enrollments, visitor_log╳security_visitors, hr_360_feedback, enps_responses╳enps_survey_responses, daily_reports VIEW oilasi.
- DATA-DRIFT (FE↔BE) — createVacancy salary/type/deadline tashlanadi.

**Soliq nuqtai nazari ZID (3 xil):** gross-only(1C) ╳ calculate-tax(12%/8% hardcode) ╳ GeneralTaxService(config) — yagona izchil soliq modeli yo'q.

**Kuchli tomonlar:** Bu 15 punktning aksariyati REAL — ayniqsa IoT-tablet (#049, SAP ME darajasi), Kanban (boards/cards/reports — to'liq CRUD + Excel/PDF + jonli data), Chat core (#058, jonli 6 xona/34 xabar + WebSocket), Director approvals/KPI (#051/052, real CQRS + 6 live SQL), CC workflow (#057, PIN-gated + tree-walk fallback), Org-structure (#060, 142 bo'lim CRUD + eksport). SaaS/Admin (#053) DB-proof bilan tasdiqlangan.

**Eng muhim muammolar:**
1. **Chat FE-BE drift (#059):** `/chat` ╳ `/hr-v2/chat` ikki prefiks + FE `getChatApiBase()=/api/chat` flat reactions/polls/thread yo'llari `/chat`da yo'q → 404 xavfi. DUPLICATE marshrut oilasi.
2. **head_user_id 18/142 (#060):** 124 bo'lim boshliqsiz → manager/approval-chain resolyutsiyasi cheklangan (vizyon #60).
3. **Security read asimmetriyasi (#046):** PPE POST real, lekin GET ppe-checks/stats/violations + fire-sensor 501.
4. **FAKE-DATA:** coordination `getCouncils` 5 ta qattiq (#057), director `avgApprovalTimeHours:0` (#051), iot `by_machine:[]` (#050), kanban pdfmake `%PDF placeholder` fallback (#056).
5. **DUPLICATE juftliklar:** iot alert-resolve POST(501)╳PATCH(real) + stale izoh (#048), material-kit-scan POST╳PATCH (#049), saas tenants/v2 (#053).

**Hisob:** REAL ko'pchilik (qurilish bosqichi → ko'p jadval bo'sh, bu kutilgan, kod real). Stub/501 asosan security read tomoni va iot resolve POST. GREEN-LIE deyarli yo'q (faqat oee by_machine qisman). Eng yaxshi: #049 IoT-tablet, #054-056 Kanban, #058 Chat core, #060 Org-structure.

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| `agents/` 16 deterministik agent | `agents/*.service.ts` (51 route `agents.controller.ts`) | `agents_audit_log`=4598, `agent_modules_registry`=20 | (Director/AI sahifalar) | yo'q | real SQL-analitika | REAL |
| supplier-agent | `agents/supplier-agent.service.ts:78` real SQL `purchase_orders` | real | — | yo'q | real | REAL |
| production-agent OEE/bottleneck | `agents/production-agent.service.ts:73,127` real SQL | real | — | yo'q | real | REAL |
| `ai-agents/` 6 LLM autonom agent | `ai-agents/presentation/ai-agents.controller.ts:107-167` | `ai_decision_log`=0 | (AI sahifalar) | yo'q | real (POST evaluate/plan/oee) | REAL |
| audit/stats, audit/:agent/decisions | `ai-agents.controller.ts:175-208` | `ai_decision_log`=0 | — | yo'q | real o'qiydi | REAL |
| GET list | `ai-agents.controller.ts:208-237` | registry'dan | — | yo'q | real | REAL |
| POST :agentId/trigger | `ai-agents.controller.ts:249` | — | — | yo'q | orkestrator ulanmagan | 501-STUB |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP'da: native "agent" yo'q; SAP Joule (Copilot) / Signavio / IBP optimizer + BTP AI Core add-on. Klassik ERP'da bu deterministik alert/workflow (yoki SAP AI add-on). | Bizda: IKKI tizim — `agents/` (16 ta deterministik SQL-analitik: OEE, bottleneck, churn, fraud, ABC) + `ai-agents/` (6 ta LLM autonom: prepress/planner/MES/QC/logistics/sales). Har LLM qaror `ai_decision_log`'ga (idempotency+audit). | Gap: `ai_decision_log`=0 (LLM agentlar hali ishlatilmagan/triggerlanmagan); manual-trigger orkestrator 501. | SAP-darajasi: 🟡 (skelet ✅ kuchli, ishlatilish 0)

### Muammolar
- DUPLICATE risk: ikki "agent" tizimi (`agents/` ╳ `ai-agents/`) — semantik ustma-ust (ikkalasida ham planning/QC/logistics/sales). Aniq chegara hujjatlashtirilmagan.
- `ai_decision_log`=0: 6 LLM agent qaror jurnali bo'sh — autonom agentlar **jonli ishlamayapti** (faqat POST orqali qo'lda chaqirilsa).
- `POST :agentId/trigger` = 501 (orkestrator ulanmagan) — "agentni ishga tushir" tugmasi yo'q.
- TEST yo'q (22 agent service, 0 spec).

### 3-qator xulosa
AI-agents skeleti kuchli: 16 deterministik SQL-agent (audit_log=4598 — real faollik) + 6 LLM autonom agent (decision-log + idempotency arxitekturasi).
Lekin LLM tomon hali "o'lik": `ai_decision_log`=0, manual-trigger 501 — agentlar avtomatik qaror qabul qilmayapti.
Ikki agent-tizimi orasidagi chegara aniq emas (DUPLICATE xavf); testlar umuman yo'q.

| orders | — (yozuvchi YO'Q) | ❌ **JADVAL YO'Q** (2026-06-06 DROP) | — | — | yo'q | ORPHAN(dropped) |
| ow_orders | — | ❌ **JADVAL YO'Q** (bor `ow_order_lines`) | — | — | yo'q | ORPHAN(yo'q) |
| sap_sales_orders (3-olam) | `integration/sap/sap.repository.ts:21,34,43` SELECT/UPDATE + fallback sales_orders | BASE, =0 | — | yo'q | real (SAP-sync olam) | REAL (3-olam) |
| orders_registry | `compatibility/orders-registry.service.ts:27,36` SELECT/INSERT | =0 | (administrativ) | yo'q | real, lekin SOTUV EMAS | REAL (boshqa domen) |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP'da: bitta kanonik Sales Document (VBAK/VBAP) — VA01/VA02/VA03; bitta haqiqat manbai. | Bizda: kanonik=`sales_orders` (BASE); `sd_sales_orders`=VIEW (bir xil data, dublikat emas — memory tasdiq); `sap_sales_orders`=3-olam (SAP-sync, sales_orders'ga fallback); `orders_registry`=mutlaqo boshqa domen (administrativ buyruq/farmoyish — "order"=buyruq, sotuv emas — nom kolliziya). | Gap: 3 ta "order-ga o'xshash" base jadval (`sales_orders`+`sap_sales_orders`+`papka_orders`); kanonik tanlov hali rasman tugamagan. | SAP-darajasi: 🔴

### Muammolar
- IKKI/UCH OLAM (tasdiq): `sales_orders` (BASE kanonik) ╳ `sap_sales_orders` (BASE, SAP-sync, alohida olam) ╳ `papka_orders` (BASE, messaging) — bir nechta base jadval.
- `sd_sales_orders`=VIEW (memory to'g'ri) — bu DUBLIKAT EMAS, sales_orders ustidagi ko'rinish.
- `orders` va `ow_orders` jadvallari live DB'da YO'Q — `orders` 2026-06-06 DROP qilingan (memory tasdiq), `ow_orders` umuman bo'lmagan (faqat `ow_order_lines`). Eski "orders olam" da'volari endi ORPHAN.
- `orders_registry` "order" so'zi tufayli adashtiradi — aslida administrativ farmoyish reestri (sotuv buyurtmasi EMAS).

### 3-qator xulosa
"Ikki olam order" endi soddalashgan: `orders` jadvali DROP qilingan, `ow_orders` umuman yo'q — kanonik `sales_orders` (BASE) + `sd_sales_orders` (uning VIEW'i).
Lekin yangi 3-olam paydo bo'ldi: `sap_sales_orders` (SAP-sync, alohida base) + `papka_orders` (messaging) — kanonik birlashtirish hali tugamagan.
`orders_registry` nom-kolliziya: u sotuv-buyurtma emas, administrativ farmoyish reestri — auditda "order olami"ga qo'shilmasligi kerak.

SAP'da: bitta Material Master (MARA + bog'liq MARC/MBEW/MARM) — MM01/MM02/MM03. | Bizda: kanonik=`material_cards` (BASE, faol — POS/warehouse/erp yozadi); `mm_materials`=**jadval yo'q** lekin MM-dashboard MRP kodi undan o'qiydi (drift); `materials`=yo'q (faqat eski yozuvlarda); `raw_materials`=alohida bog'liq lug'at (dublikat emas — memory tasdiq). | Gap: MM modulining MRP qatlami yo'q jadvalga (`mm_materials`) tayanadi → silently fail; material-master birlashtirish chala. | SAP-darajasi: 🔴

Asosiy muammo `mm_materials`: jadval yo'q, lekin MM dashboard MRP kodi undan o'qiydi → silently fail (KOD↔DB drift, eng zararli yashirin nosozlik).
SAP material-master birligi uchun MM-dashboard MRP qatlami `material_cards`'ga ko'chirilishi yoki `mm_materials` yaratilishi shart.

Haqiqiy ikki-olam = `warehouse_stock` ╳ `stocks` (WMS receiveFg yozadi) — bu memory FIX4 deferral'ining ildizi, owner hali kanonik jadval tanlamagan.

**Eng kuchli (🟢):** [072] Material-balance — SAP MB52/MB51 darajasiga eng yaqin, smoke-test + jonli data (`material_movements`=5).

**Eng yaxshi yashirin gavhar:** [063] Layer-formula (vizyon#7 miya — hech narsa hardcoded emas) va [066] Croston/TSB demand-forecast — ikkalasi ham SAP IBP darajasidagi algoritm, lekin ORPHAN (FE'ga ulanmagan).

[062][069][074] ta'sirlangan.

**Ochiq GREEN-LIE:** [067] `acceptDecision`+`blockMaterial` DB yozmaydi (faqat log); [061] FE `history` kontrakti buzuq (`items` vs `history`).

**Raqam tuzatish:** [071] "623 route" → jonli 459 (compat 343 + remaining 116); eski son bo'rttirilgan.

**Ikki olam holati (073/074/075):** asl da'volar SODDALASHGAN — `orders`/`ow_orders`/`materials`/`mm_materials`/`wms_stock` jadvallari endi YO'Q (DROP yoki hech bo'lmagan); haqiqiy ochiq ikki-olam faqat **stock**: `warehouse_stock` ╳ `stocks` (FIX4 deferral). Order'da yangi 3-olam `sap_sales_orders` paydo bo'lgan.

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| Kanonik GL `entries` | drizzle-gl-posting.repo.ts:57-95 `insertJournal` (atomik tx) | `entries` 0 | FinanceExtended/GLDocuments (lekin `/api/fi/gl-documents`ga) | yo'q | REAL yozadi, lekin chaqiruvchi yo'q | REAL (UNEXERCISED) |
| `entries` POS ko'prik | gl-posting-log.repository.ts:146-153 `postMovementToLedger` INSERT INTO entries | `entries` 0 | yo'q | yo'q | gl_account_mappings (6 qator) bo'sh emas, lekin chaqiruvchi yo'q | GREEN-LIE (yozardi, lekin hech kim chaqirmaydi) |
| Ikkilamchi GL `gl_journal_entries` | drizzle-finance-invoice.repo.ts:202 `saveGlEntry` INSERT INTO gl_journal_entries; hr/payroll/drizzle-hr-payroll.repo.ts:97 insert | `gl_journal_entries` 0, `gl_lines` 0 | FinanceDashboard (trial-balance) | yo'q | 8+ joy SELECT, 2 joy INSERT — parallel olam | DUPLICATE (ikkinchi GL olami) |
| POS GL log `pos_gl_posting_log` | gl-posting-log.repository.ts:15 `insertLog`; pos-movement-status.service.ts:212 (INLINE) | `pos_gl_posting_log` 0 | yo'q | yo'q | AWAITING_REVIEW status, Finance qo'lda tasdiqlaydi | REAL (UCHINCHI GL olami) |
| POS auto-GL `pos_gl_postings` | auto-gl-posting.repository.ts:82 INSERT INTO pos_gl_postings | `pos_gl_postings` 0 | yo'q | yo'q | posted_by='AI' hardcode | REAL (TO'RTINCHI GL olami) |
| Stok GL `stock_gl_postings` | integration-extended-mro.repo.ts:202 (faqat SELECT) | `stock_gl_postings` 0 | MRO/integration | yo'q | faqat o'qish, yozuvchi YO'Q | ORPHAN |
| `stock_movement_gl_postings`, `gl_documents`, `fi_gl_documents`, `payroll_journal_entries` | tarqoq | barchasi 0 | qisman | yo'q | — | ORPHAN/DUPLICATE |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Bitta universal jurnal (ACDOCA — "Universal Journal"). Har biznes hodisa (sotuv, POS, ish haqi, stok) → BIR jadvalga debit/kredit leg'lar bilan. Bitta haqiqat manbai, real-time trial balance.
- **Bizda:** **KAMIDA 7 ta parallel GL jadvali** — `entries` (kanonik), `gl_journal_entries`+`gl_lines` (invoice/payroll), `pos_gl_posting_log` (POS inline), `pos_gl_postings` (POS auto-AI), `stock_gl_postings`, `stock_movement_gl_postings`, `gl_documents`/`fi_gl_documents`. Hech biri bir-biriga FK bilan bog'lanmagan. Barchasi 0 qator.
- **Gap:** Universal jurnal yo'q; POS olami (`pos_gl_*`) Finance olamiga (`entries`) hech qachon ko'prik qilmaydi — `postMovementToLedger` bor, lekin uni chaqiruvchi kod yo'q (inline path `gl_posting_log`ga yozadi, `entries`ga emas). Trial balance ikki-uch joydan o'qiydi.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🔴

### Muammolar (5-tomon mos kelmagan)
- **KOD↔DB drift:** `entries` (17 ustun) va `gl_entries` (17 ustun) — ikki bir xil jadval. Kod `entries`ga yozadi, `gl_entries` butunlay o'lik (yozuvchi yo'q, _audit/q.cjs: 0 qator).
- **GREEN-LIE ko'prik:** `gl-posting-log.repository.ts:146` POS→`entries` INSERT bor, lekin chaqiruvchi pos-movement-status.service.ts:212 o'rniga **inline `gl_posting_log`ga** yozadi (satr 203-226 izoh: "INLINE — NOT via the dead PosMovementCompletedEvent"). Demak `entries`ga POS dan hech narsa tushmaydi.
- **DB↔FOYDA:** 7 jadvalning hammasi 0 qator — bironta GL yozuvi hech qachon yaratilmagan. gl_account_mappings 6 qator (sozlama bor), lekin mapping ishlatilmaydi.
- **FE drift:** GLDocuments.tsx → `/api/fi/gl-documents` (fi.controller.ts:167) — `gl_journal_entries` olamiga; `entries` kanonik olamini FE umuman ko'rsatmaydi.
- **TEST:** gl-posting uchun BE spec YO'Q; faqat GLDocuments.smoke.test.tsx (FE smoke).

### 3-qator xulosa
Yetti parallel GL jadvali bir-biriga bog'lanmagan, hammasi bo'sh; SAP'ning universal jurnal tamoyiliga mutlaqo zid. `entries` kanonik deb e'lon qilingan, lekin POS uni inline `gl_posting_log` bilan chetlab o'tadi → ko'prik o'lik. Birorta GL yozuvi jonli yaratilmagan (UNEXERCISED).

| `attendance` (drizzle ORM) | drizzle-attendance.repo.ts:54 db.insert(attendance) | 0 | AttendanceMonitorPage | yo'q | ORM yozadi (status='present' hardcode) | DUPLICATE |
| `attendance_logs` | manager.repo.ts:116/154/175, events.repo.ts:153/226 (faqat SELECT+JOIN) | 0 | — | yo'q | faqat o'qiladi, yozuvchi topilmadi | ORPHAN (o'qish) |
| `hr_tz2_daily_attendance` | territory-log.repository.ts:219 db.insert + 228 update | 0 | territory.gateway | yo'q | territory enter/exit yozadi | REAL (alohida olam) |
| `hr_ai_attendance` | iot-agent.service.ts:60 INSERT (Drizzle schema'da yo'q — raw) | 0 | — | yo'q | kamera face yozadi; director-agent.service.ts:92 o'qiydi | REAL (alohida olam) |

- **SAP'da:** SAP HCM Time Management — bitta `PA2002`/`CATSDB` time cluster; barcha clock-in/out, territory, kamera bir time-evaluation pipeline'iga oqadi. Odoo: bitta `hr.attendance` jadvali.
- **Bizda:** **9+ davomat jadvali**, 4 xil yozuvchi bilan: `attendance` (3 xil ustun-nomli yozuvchi — telegram, HR-base, ORM), `hr_tz2_daily_attendance` (territory), `hr_ai_attendance` (kamera), `attendance_logs` (faqat o'qiladi). Hech biri konsolidatsiya qilinmagan.
- **Gap:** Yagona davomat manbai yo'q; kim qaysi jadvalga yozishi tasodifiy (yo'l telegram'mi, kamera'mi, ORM'mi). KPI hisoblovchi (legacy-kpi.helpers.ts:76) `attendance_logs`dan o'qiydi — lekin unga **hech kim yozmaydi** → KPI doim bo'sh.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🔴

### Muammolar
- **KOD↔KOD drift (bir jadval, uch ustun-nom):** `attendance.date` (manager.repo:201) ╳ `attendance.attendance_date` (drizzle-hr-base:256) ╳ `attendance.checkIn` (drizzle-attendance:54) — bir jadvalga uch xil sxema bilan yozish → ba'zilari crash beradi.
- **O'qish↔Yozish uzilishi:** KPI/manager hisobotlar `attendance_logs`dan o'qiydi (5+ joy), lekin INSERT INTO attendance_logs hech qayerda yo'q → hisobotlar abadiy bo'sh.
- **DB↔FOYDA:** 9 jadval ham 0 qator — bironta davomat jonli yozilmagan.
- **TEST:** attendance uchun bironta spec YO'Q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Davomat 9+ jadvalga bo'lingan, 4 mustaqil yozuvchi har xil jadval/ustunga yozadi. `attendance_logs` (KPI manbai)ga yozuvchi yo'q → KPI o'lik. SAP'ning yagona time-cluster modeliga mutlaqo zid; barchasi 0 qator.

## [078] ZERO-LISTENER event audit (emit bor, listener yo'q)

### 5-tomonlama jadval (asosiy dead-letter emitlar)

| event nomi | KOD emit fayl:satr | @OnEvent bormi? | Bus | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `iot.anomaly` | iot-agent.service.ts:41,45 | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 (AgentBus orqali) | IoT anomaliya hech kim eshitmaydi | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `production.delayed` | production-agent.service.ts:81 | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | kechikish alarmsiz | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `quality.defect_rising` | quality-agent.service.ts:47 | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | sifat tendentsiya alarmsiz | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `hr.payroll.calculated` | calculate-payroll.handler.ts:96 | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | ish haqi hisoblandi → hech kim | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `payroll.period.closed` | payroll.service.ts:94 | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | davr yopildi → GL/notif yo'q | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `pos.gl.auto_posted` | pos-gl-auto.listener.ts:109 | YO'Q (izohda "by design") | EventEmitter2 | GL yozildi → Finance bildirishnoma yo'q | ZERO-LISTENER (atayin) |
| `pos.requisition.*` (5 ta: submitted/approved/rejected/cancelled/fulfilled) | pos requisition service | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | talabnoma oqimi tinglanmaydi | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `stock.critical`, `warehouse.roll_low`, `procurement.delivery_risk` | agent servislar | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | kritik stok alarmsiz | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `rbac.permission.changed` | rbac service | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | RBAC keshi invalidatsiya bo'lmaydi | ZERO-LISTENER |
| `employee.created`, `hr.low_performance`, `crm.hot_leads_found`, `director.briefing_sent`, `camera.rejection.counted`, `mes.machine.resumed`, `interview.*`, `sales.copilot.pdf_dispatch`, `security.alert`, `security.emergency`, `territory.event`, `ai.*` | tarqoq | YO'Q | EventEmitter2 | — | ZERO-LISTENER |

### Hisob (skript bilan tasdiqlangan)
`comm -23 emits onevents`: **44 ta emit-topic** uchun `@OnEvent` listener yo'q. Ulardan **~22 tasi WebSocket gateway topiklari** (`message`, `new_message`, `room_joined`, `connected`, `unread_count`, `user_typing`, `rooms_list`, `session.joined`, `direct_room`, `messages_list`, `error` — bu mijoz tomonga `socket.emit`, server `@OnEvent` kerak emas → SOXTA-POZITIV emas). **~22 tasi haqiqiy dead-letter** (yuqoridagi jadval).

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Event Mesh / BO Events — har emit qilingan event yo abadiy persist (outbox) yoki kamida bitta subscription'ga ega; "orphan event" monitoring tomonidan ushlanadi.
- **Bizda:** ~22 biznes-event hech kim eshitmaydi (AgentBus ham oxir-oqibat shu EventEmitter2'ga emit qiladi — agent-event-bus.service.ts:24 tasdiqlangan, alohida bus emas).
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🔴

### Muammolar
- **AgentBus = soxta izolyatsiya:** `agent-event-bus.service.ts:24` `this.emitter.emit(eventName)` — bir xil global EventEmitter2. Demak agent eventlari ham real dead-letter (alohida bus emas).
- **payroll.period.closed o'lik:** ish haqi davri yopilganda GL posting/bildirishnoma trigger bo'lishi kerak (SAP'da FI hujjat yaratiladi) — bizda hech kim eshitmaydi.

### 3-qator xulosa
44 emit-topic listenersiz; 22 tasi WS-client topiklari (normal), 22 tasi haqiqiy biznes dead-letter. IoT anomaliya, ish-haqi-yopildi, kritik-stok, RBAC-o'zgardi — barchasi havoga emit qilinadi. SAP Event Mesh modelidan juda uzoq.

| `hr.candidate.added` | AI listener | YO'Q (event-bridge.service.ts:65 defensiv map) | nomzod AI hech qachon ishlamaydi | ORPHAN-LISTENER |
| `finance.invoice.created` | AI listener | YO'Q (event-bridge.service.ts:66 defensiv map) | invoice AI o'lik | ORPHAN-LISTENER |
| `pos.gl.approved` / `pos.gl.rejected` | pos gl listener | YO'Q emit topildi | GL tasdiq oqimi tinglanadi, lekin trigger yo'q | ORPHAN-LISTENER |
| `pos.movement.data.qc_approved/qc_rejected/qc_rework/ai_processing/approved/cancelled` | pos.events.ts | qisman (faqat completed/created/pending emit qilinadi) | QC oqimi listeneri kutadi, emit yo'q | ORPHAN-LISTENER |
| `attendance.late_arrival/early_departure/employee_blocked` | hr listener | YO'Q (faqat territory_enter/exit emit) | kechikish listeneri trigger'siz | ORPHAN-LISTENER |
| `document.rejected`, `incident.created`, `email.account.disable`, `employee.absence.day1/day2`, `candidate.applied/stage_changed`, `telegram.late_reason_received` | tarqoq | YO'Q | — | ORPHAN-LISTENER |

### Hisob
`comm -13 emits onevents`: **28 ta `@OnEvent` topic** uchun mos emit YO'Q. Bularning ko'pi **CQRS event-class** orqali keladi (EventBridge.EVENT_NAME_MAP class-nom→string-topic ko'prik, event-bridge.service.ts:22-81) — ya'ni emit `eventBus.publish(new XEvent())` shaklida bo'lib, string-emit grep'ga tushmaydi. Lekin **bir nechtasi (crm.lead.created, hr.candidate.added, finance.invoice.created)** EventBridge izohida "dead-letter today, kept defensively" deb belgilangan — ya'ni publisher umuman yo'q.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Event nomi yagona registry'da (BO event katalogi); name-drift compile-time'da ushlanadi.
- **Bizda:** Uch parallel mexanizm (CQRS EventBus ╳ EventEmitter2 ERP_EVENTS namespace ╳ EventEmitter2 string-topic). EventBridge ularni qo'lda ko'prik qiladi (event-bridge.service.ts:1-15 izoh) — har yangi event uchun map'ni qo'lda yangilash kerak, unutilsa → silent drop.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (ko'prik bor, lekin qo'lda va to'liq emas)

### Muammolar
- **Uch mexanizm, qo'lda ko'prik:** event-bridge.service.ts:6-9 "without this bridge, those cross-mechanism trigger pairs silently drop" — o'zi tan oladi.
- **Defensiv o'lik listenerlar:** event-bridge.service.ts:63 "all three are dead-letter today, kept defensively" — crm.lead.created/hr.candidate.added/finance.invoice.created.
- **LEVELS drift (RBAC bilan bog'liq):** permission.guard.ts:14 `LEVELS=['NONE','READ','READ_PLUS','WRITE','FULL']` — lekin DB'da `LIMITED` access_level bor (32 qator) → `indexOf('LIMITED')=-1` → noto'g'ri taqqos.

### 3-qator xulosa
28 listener mos emitsiz; ko'pi CQRS-class orqali keladi, lekin 3+ tasi (crm/hr/finance AI) butunlay publisher'siz "dead-letter, defensiv". Uch parallel event mexanizm qo'lda EventBridge map bilan ko'prik qilingan → har yangi event unutilsa silent drop. SAP'ning yagona event registry'sidan uzoq.

| jadval | qator | yozuvchi (KOD) | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|
| `gl_entries` | 0 | YO'Q (kod faqat `entries`ga yozadi) | ORPHAN (dublikat `entries`) |
| `gl_lines` | 0 | YO'Q topildi | ORPHAN |
| `stock_gl_postings` | 0 | faqat SELECT (integration-extended-mro.repo.ts:202) | ORPHAN |
| `attendance_records` | 0 | YO'Q (README'da "canonical" lekin INSERT yo'q) | ORPHAN |
| `audit_trail_log` | 0 | YO'Q (audit_logs ishlatiladi) | ORPHAN (dublikat) |
| `cc_audit_trail` | 0 | YO'Q | ORPHAN |
| `ai_decision_log` | 0 | YO'Q | ORPHAN |
| `pos_stock_ledger`, `stock_ledger`, `stock_movement_gl_postings`, `daily_attendance_summary`, `security_attendance`, `worker_session_events` | 0 | YO'Q yoki faqat o'qish | ORPHAN |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Har jadval data-model'da hujjatlangan; ishlatilmagan jadval bo'lmaydi (DDIC). Odoo: model = jadval, 1:1.
- **Bizda:** 952 jadval, faqat ~10 tasida data. Yuzlab jadval 0 qator + yozuvchi yo'q. Lekin DIQQAT (Schema-barrel precedence memory): ko'pi "ADD-ONLY superset" — Drizzle stub jadval kanonikni ustun qiladi, jonli DB add-only o'sgan → 0-qator demak orphan demaslik mumkin (qurilish bosqichi).
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🔴 (jadval ko'pligi, hujjatsiz)

- **DB↔KOD:** `gl_entries`/`gl_lines` `entries` bilan dublikat — kod faqat `entries`ga yozadi.
- **Hujjat↔KOD yolg'on:** `attendance_records` README.md:62'da "canonical daily clock-in/out" deyilgan, lekin INSERT topilmadi.

952 jadvalning aksariyati 0 qator; o'nlab tasdiqlangan orphan (gl_entries, gl_lines, stock_gl_postings, attendance_records, 2 audit dublikat). Qurilish bosqichi sababli ba'zilari "hali" bo'lishi mumkin, lekin yozuvchisiz orphanlar (gl_entries, audit_trail_log) toza axlat. SAP DDIC tartibidan uzoq.

## [081] domain_events / outbox (nega ishlatilmaydi)

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Outbox yozuvchi | create-order.handler.ts:108-113 `insertBatch(outboxRows, tx)` (header+items+outbox BIR tx) | `domain_events` 0 | — | yo'q | atomik, to'g'ri qurilgan | REAL (UNEXERCISED) |
| Outbox repo | outbox.repository.ts:48 `db.insert(domain_events).values(...)` | 0 | — | yo'q | real INSERT | REAL |
| Outbox publisher | outbox-publisher.service.ts:31 `@Interval 10s` → fetchUnpublished→emit→markPublished | 0 | — | yo'q | real cron, ishlaydi | REAL (UNEXERCISED) |
| markPublished | outbox.repository.ts:96 UPDATE published_at (o'chirmaydi) | — | — | yo'q | published satr qoladi | REAL |

### Nega 0 qator? (TASDIQLANGAN)
`sales_orders`=12 qator BOR, lekin `domain_events`=0. Tekshiruv: 12 buyurtmaning `created_at` bir xil seed-vaqt (`...09:00:59.063Z`) → ular **CreateOrderHandler orqali EMAS, to'g'ridan SEED qilingan**. markPublished o'chirmaydi (UPDATE qiladi), shuning uchun agar handler ishlaganida `domain_events`da published satr qolardi. 0 qator = handler **jonli hech qachon ishlamagan**.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAPّda:** Outbox/Event Mesh — har transaction event'i persist + guaranteed delivery (transactional outbox pattern).
- **Bizda:** Pattern TO'G'RI implementatsiya qilingan (atomik tx + 10s drain + idempotent markPublished). Lekin **birorta domain event jonli yaratilmagan** — chunki order create FE'dan o'tmagan, seed bilan to'ldirilgan.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (kod SAP-darajasi, lekin amalda hech qachon ishlamagan)

### Muammolar
- **DB↔FOYDA:** Mukammal outbox kodi, 0 jonli foydalanish. "Ishlaydi-yu bo'sh".
- **TEST:** Outbox uchun BE spec YO'Q (`*outbox*.spec.ts` topilmadi) — kritik infratuzilma testlanmagan.
- **Faqat order create yozadi:** Outbox faqat SD create-order.handler bilan bog'langan; boshqa modullar (payroll, POS, HR) outbox'ni ishlatmaydi → EventEmitter2'ga to'g'ridan emit qiladi (kafolatsiz).

### 3-qator xulosa
Transactional outbox to'g'ri qurilgan (atomik tx, 10s publisher, idempotent) — bu kod SAP-darajasi. Lekin `domain_events`=0: 12 buyurtma seed qilingan, handler jonli ishlamagan → outbox amalda hech qachon ishlamagan. Faqat SD modulida; qolgan modullar kafolatsiz EventEmitter2 ishlatadi.

## [082] EventBridge / CQRS oqimi

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | ishlaydi? | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EventBridge (CQRS→EventEmitter2) | event-bridge.service.ts:92-121 `eventBus.subscribe` → EVENT_NAME_MAP → re-emit | HA (onModuleInit) | yo'q | class-nom → string-topic ko'prik | REAL |
| EVENT_NAME_MAP | event-bridge.service.ts:22-81 (~40 map) | HA | yo'q | qo'lda yangilanadigan jadval | REAL (FRAGILE) |
| CQRS EventBus publish | sales-order.aggregate.ts, qc/pp listenerlar | HA | qisman | typed event nashr | REAL |
| Outbox→EventEmitter2 | outbox-publisher.service.ts:40 | HA (UNEXERCISED) | yo'q | — | REAL |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Bitta event infratuzilma (Event Mesh) — manbaa-iste'molchi loosely coupled, registry-driven.
- **Bizda:** **Uch mexanizm** (CQRS EventBus ╳ EventEmitter2 ERP_EVENTS ╳ EventEmitter2 string) + outbox = **to'rtinchi**. EventBridge ularni ko'prik qiladi, lekin qo'lda EVENT_NAME_MAP bilan.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡

### Muammolar
- **FRAGILE map:** Har yangi CQRS event uchun event-bridge.service.ts:23 map'ga qo'lda yozish shart; unutilsa string-listener jim qoladi (xizmat o'zi izohlaydi: satr 8-9).
- **To'rt mexanizm bir vaqtda:** create-order.handler.ts:132-134 izoh: "Listeners using @OnEvent may fire twice ... Handlers are expected to be idempotent" — outbox + to'g'ridan emit ikki marta trigger qiladi.
- **TEST yo'q:** event-bridge uchun spec topilmadi.

### 3-qator xulosa
EventBridge real ishlaydi (CQRS→EventEmitter2 ko'prik), lekin qo'lda EVENT_NAME_MAP jadval bilan — fragile. To'rt parallel event mexanizm bir vaqtda, ba'zi listenerlar ikki marta trigger bo'ladi (idempotency'ga tayanadi). SAP'ning yagona Event Mesh'idan uzoq, lekin ishlaydi.

## [085] Auth / RBAC (position_permissions 1380)

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| position_permissions matritsa | admin/position-permissions/*.ts | **1380** (92 pos × 15 modul × 5 daraja) | PositionPermissions admin | yo'q | to'liq matritsa qurilgan | REAL |
| PermissionGuard | permission.guard.ts:63-81 | o'qiydi | — | yo'q | faqat `@RequirePermission`li route'da ishlaydi | REAL (KAM QAMROV) |
| @RequirePermission qamrovi | 339 controller'dan **faqat 32 tasida** | — | — | — | 90% route position_permissions'ni e'tiborsiz qoldiradi | GREEN-LIE (matritsa qurilgan, lekin ishlatilmaydi) |
| RolesGuard | roles.guard.ts:32-61 (3 rol: manager/super_admin/director) | — | — | yo'q | real enforce; admin bypass | REAL |
| 4 global guard | app.module.ts:194-197 (Jwt/Roles/Sod/Permission) | — | — | qisman | real, fail-secure | REAL |
| erp_roles/role_menus/role_movement_permissions/role_dashboard_widgets/role_ui_configs | — | barchasi **0** | — | yo'q | RBAC sozlama jadvallari bo'sh | ORPHAN |

### Tasdiq (DB)
position_permissions=1380; access_level distro: NONE 919, READ 306, FULL 79, READ_PLUS 44, LIMITED 32. 92 distinct position, 15 modul. users: manager 27, super_admin 3, director 1. @RequirePermission grep: 32/339 controller.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** PFCG roles + authorization objects — har transaction auth-check bilan; role-menu, field-level RBAC.
- **Bizda:** Boy position_permissions matritsa (1380) QURILGAN, lekin PermissionGuard faqat `@RequirePermission` dekorli route'da tekshiradi → **307/339 controller matritsani umuman ishlatmaydi** (faqat coarse 3-rol RolesGuard). erp_roles/role_menus jadvallari bo'sh.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (struktura bor, qamrov yo'q)

### Muammolar
- **GREEN-LIE matritsa:** 1380 qatorli RBAC matritsa qurilgan, lekin 90% route uni o'qimaydi (permission.guard.ts:64-65: `@RequirePermission` yo'q → return true).
- **LEVELS drift:** permission.guard.ts:14 `LEVELS` massivida `LIMITED` YO'Q, lekin DB'da 32 qator LIMITED → `indexOf=-1` noto'g'ri taqqoslash.
- **Faqat 3 rol:** users'da manager/super_admin/director — manager 27 ta keng rol; granular position-level RBAC amalda 32 route'da.
- **Sozlama jadvallari bo'sh:** erp_roles, role_menus, role_movement_permissions, role_dashboard_widgets, role_ui_configs — hammasi 0.

### 3-qator xulosa
1380-qatorli position_permissions matritsa to'liq qurilgan (92 pos × 15 modul × 5 daraja) — struktura SAP-yo'nalishida. Lekin PermissionGuard faqat 32/339 route'da tekshiradi → 90% route coarse 3-rol RolesGuard'ga tayanadi; matritsa amalda dekorativ. LEVELS massivi LIMITED'ni o'tkazib yuboradi (drift). Global guardlar fail-secure (yaxshi).

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| metrik | qiymat (DB) | SAP kutilgan | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|
| Jadval soni | 952 base table | modul-proporsional | FAKE-DATA (shish) |
| FK (foreign key) | **193** | ~jadval soniga yaqin | 🔴 juda kam |
| PK (primary key) | 950 | =952 | OK |
| Index | 1807 | — | OK |

`SELECT count FK=193, PK=950, index=1807` for 952 tables.

- **SAP'da:** To'liq normalizatsiya, FK referential integrity (yoki ABAP-level), DDIC izchillik. Har relation FK bilan.
- **Bizda:** 952 jadval, faqat 193 FK (20% jadval'da FK bog'lanish). Ko'p "ikki olam" (GL 7, attendance 9, audit 3, sales_orders╳orders) FK bilan bog'lanmagan. Memory: Phase 2'da 7 FK qo'shilgan (sales_orders'ga), lekin 12 uuid FK hali ochiq.

### 3-qator xulosa
952 jadval, lekin faqat 193 FK (20% qamrov) — referential integrity SAP-darajasidan juda uzoq. GL×7, attendance×9, audit×3 parallel olamlar FK'siz → normalizatsiya buzuq. Jadval shishi (952 vs ~10 real-data) qurilish bosqichi + add-only supersetdan.

## [087] Tranzaksiya / atomiklik (pul / stok atomikmi)

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD fayl:satr | atomikmi? | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GL jurnal insert | drizzle-gl-posting.repo.ts:71 `db.transaction` (barcha leg birga) | HA ✅ | yarim-jurnal yo'q | REAL (atomik) |
| Order create + items + outbox | create-order.handler.ts:86-113 BIR tx | HA ✅ | header+item+outbox atomik | REAL (atomik) |
| Order items insert | drizzle-sales-order.repo.ts:52-75 (tx-aware) | HA (tx berilsa) | — | REAL |
| **POS movement → stok + ledger + GL** | pos-movement-status.service.ts:181-226 (ketma-ket await, **tx YO'Q**) | **YO'Q ❌** | stok yozildi, GL fail → yarim holat | GREEN-LIE (atomiksiz) |
| db.transaction umumiy qamrov | 32 fayl (339 controller'dan) | qisman | — | KAM QAMROV |

### Tasdiq (KOD)
- GL: drizzle-gl-posting.repo.ts:71 `await db.transaction(async (tx) => {...})` — atomik.
- POS: pos-movement-status.service.ts:198 `stockLedger.recordConfirmation` → 212 `glRepo.insertLog` — **alohida await, surrounding transaction yo'q**. Izoh (satr 203) faqat double-write'dan qochishni tushuntiradi, atomiklikni emas.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** LUW (Logical Unit of Work) — material harakat + GL hujjat + stok yangilanish BIR COMMIT WORK'da. Yarim-post imkonsiz.
- **Bizda:** GL-jurnal va order-create atomik (yaxshi). Lekin POS movement completion stok-loop + stock-ledger + GL-log **alohida** yoziladi, tx yo'q → GL insert fail bo'lsa stok allaqachon yozilgan = nomuvozanat.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (yarim — pul tomoni atomik, stok+GL emas)

### Muammolar
- **POS atomiklik buzuq:** pos-movement-status.service.ts:181-226 — `employeeLedger.addEntry` (loop) + `stockLedger.recordConfirmation` + `glRepo.insertLog` ketma-ket, tx'siz. Oraliq xato → yarim holat.
- **GL pul tomoni yaxshi:** insertJournal atomik (mirrors saveBudget).
- **Qamrov:** 32/339 controller tx ishlatadi — ko'p multi-write oqim atomiksiz.

### 3-qator xulosa
GL-jurnal va order-create+outbox to'g'ri atomik (db.transaction) — pul tomoni SAP-yo'nalishida. Lekin POS movement stok+ledger+GL alohida await, surrounding tx yo'q → GL fail bo'lsa stok yozilgan holda qoladi (nomuvozanat). SAP LUW (COMMIT WORK) modeli faqat qisman.

|---|---|---|---|
| sql.raw(o'zgaruvchi) | admin-extra.repo.ts:122 (literal SELECT), aisha/compare-periods.tool.ts:78 (whitelist `meta.table`) | YO'Q (whitelist/literal) | REAL (xavfsiz) |
| 4 global guard | app.module.ts:194-197 Jwt+Roles+Sod+Permission | fail-secure | REAL |
| @Public route | 16 fayl (auth, ecommerce, bot-gateway, website-media, iot-tablet) | tahlil qilingan | REAL (atayin) |
| RolesGuard | roles.guard.ts:45 (rol yo'q → ForbiddenException) | fail-secure | REAL |
| ISO_DATE_RE guard | compare-periods.tool.ts:63-64 (LLM date validatsiya) | defense-in-depth | REAL |

### Tasdiq (KOD)
Ikki `sql.raw(o'zgaruvchi)` tekshirildi: admin-extra.repo.ts:122 — literal string `SELECT DISTINCT table_name FROM audit_logs`; compare-periods.tool.ts:78 — `meta.table` yopiq ALLOWED whitelist'dan (sales_orders/production_orders/qc_inspections), user input emas. Ikkalasi xavfsiz.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Authorization objects + SQL injection ABAP'da deyarli imkonsiz (Open SQL). Audit majburiy.
- **Bizda:** SQL injection yaxshi mitigatsiya (parametrli sql\`\${}\`, sql.raw faqat whitelist). 4 global fail-secure guard. @Public'lar atayin (memory pentest: iot-tablet/storage tuzatilgan).
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (auth yaxshi, RBAC qamrovi kam — [085])

### Muammolar
- **SQL-inj YO'Q:** Kod konvensiyalari (Qoida B) yaxshi qo'llangan — sql.raw faqat literal/whitelist.
- **RBAC qamrov [085]:** Position-level permission 90% route'da tekshirilmaydi — granular auth-bypass emas, lekin coarse.
- **Sensitive:** memory pentest — salary leak YO'Q, manager→admin escalation YO'Q (oldingi sessiyada tasdiqlangan).

### 3-qator xulosa
Xavfsizlik asoslari mustahkam: SQL injection yo'q (parametrli + whitelist sql.raw), 4 global fail-secure guard, @Public'lar atayin va tahlil qilingan. Asosiy zaiflik — RBAC granularligining 90% route'da yo'qligi ([085]), bu auth-bypass emas, balki coarse-grained nazorat. Pentest memory: salary/escalation leak yo'q.

## [090] Performance (N+1 / katta jadval / sekin)

### 5-tomonlama jadval

| feature | KOD | DB ta'sir | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|
| Eng katta jadval | agents_audit_log 4598, daily_reports 3150, audit_logs 9342 | kichik | OK (data yo'q) |
| N+1 (controller) | chat-advanced.controller.ts (CLAUDE.md Qoida 6: "N+1 loop → getBulkUnread"), order items loop (drizzle-sales-order.repo.ts:58 INSERT loop) | — | 🟡 ba'zi N+1 |
| Index qamrov | 1807 index / 952 jadval | — | OK (~2/jadval) |
| typedExecute cast | pos repos (CLAUDE.md Qoida 16: 15+ cast) | — | kod sifati |

### Tasdiq (DB)
pg_stat_user_tables eng katta: agents_audit_log 4598, daily_reports 3150. DB deyarli bo'sh → performance hozir muammo EMAS, lekin pattern xavfli.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** HANA in-memory, CDS view, code-pushdown; N+1 ABAP'da SELECT...FOR ALL ENTRIES bilan oldini olinadi.
- **Bizda:** DB kichik (qurilish bosqichi) → hozir sekin emas. Lekin N+1 patternlar mavjud (chat-advanced bulk-unread, order-items insert loop satr 58). Scale'da muammo bo'ladi.
- **SAP-darajasi:** 🟡 (hozir OK, pattern xavfli)

### Muammolar
- **N+1 patternlar:** CLAUDE.md o'zi sanaydi — chat-advanced.controller (per-user unread loop), pp-intelligence MRP matrix. order-items satr 58 loop-insert (batch emas).
- **Scale sinovlanmagan:** Eng katta jadval 9342 qator — real yuk hech qachon sinovlanmagan.
- **Index:** 1807 index — qamrov yetarli ko'rinadi (DB bo'sh, profil yo'q).

### 3-qator xulosa
Performance hozir muammo EMAS — DB qurilish bosqichida deyarli bo'sh (eng katta jadval 9342 qator). Lekin N+1 patternlar kodda mavjud (chat-advanced bulk-unread, order-items insert-loop) va scale'da xavfli. SAP HANA code-pushdown darajasidan uzoq; index qamrov yetarli ko'rinadi.

## YAKUNIY XULOSA (15 punkt)

**Eng kritik tema — "IKKI/KO'P OLAM" sindromi:** GL×7, attendance×9, audit×3, order×2 parallel jadval, FK'siz, konsolidatsiyasiz. SAP'ning yagona-haqiqat-manbai (Universal Journal, Time Cluster, Change Document) tamoyiliga tubdan zid.

**Eng yaxshi tomon:** Audit-log ([088]) HAQIQATAN ishlaydi (9342 qator — DB'dagi eng to'la jadval); xavfsizlik asoslari ([089]) mustahkam (SQL-inj yo'q, 4 fail-secure guard); outbox/GL-atomiklik kodi to'g'ri qurilgan (lekin UNEXERCISED).

**Eng yashirin yolg'on:** position_permissions 1380-qatorli RBAC matritsa qurilgan, lekin 90% route uni o'qimaydi (GREEN-LIE); transactional outbox mukammal, lekin domain_events=0 (jonli hech ishlamagan); POS→entries GL ko'prik mavjud, lekin inline path bilan chetlab o'tilgan.

> Tahlilchi (QAT'IY READ-ONLY). Hech bir endpoint jonli chaqirilmadi — statik kod + read-only grep + DB SELECT (`_audit/q.cjs`).

> VERIFY-DON'T-TRUST: har da'vo fayl:satr yoki DB natija bilan tasdiqlangan.

- 845 `.tsx` sahifa fayli (`pages/*.tsx`), 211 `*.smoke.test.tsx` (render-only smoke; chuqur test EMAS).
- YAGONA HTTP qatlam: `lib/api-request.ts` + `lib/queryClient.ts` (`apiRequest`) — Rule 21 raw `fetch` taqiqlaydi. POS alohida `pos-monitor/api/pos-monitor.api.ts` (`posReq`, ERP cookie SSO).
- Mock massiv deyarli yo'q: `grep "const mock... = ["` faqat 1 test faylida. Sahifalarning aksariyati REAL `apiRequest` ishlatadi.
- DB build-bosqichida bo'sh: `sd_customers`=9, `sales_orders`=12, `crm_leads`=5, `crm_deals`=0, `marketing_campaigns`=0, `design_orders`=0, `qc_reclamations`=0, `pos_movements`=2, `fi_invoices`=0, `employees`=30, `users`=31.

## [091] FE CRM/SD pages (drift + forma save Q-43)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SD mijozlar CRUD | `SDCustomers.tsx:191,208,219` POST/PUT/DELETE `/api/sd/customers` → `sd-customers.controller.ts:188,202,219` | `sd_customers`=9 | `/sd/customers` | `SDCustomers.smoke.test.tsx` | REAL foyda — to'liq CRUD + rhf+zod | REAL |
| SD savdo buyurtma | `SDSalesOrders.tsx` → `/api/sd/sales-orders` | `sales_orders`=12 | `/sd/sales-orders` | smoke | REAL (Phase 4 fan-out spine ulangan) | REAL |
| SD kotirovka | CRMRoutes:62 `/sd/quotations`→SDSalesQuotes (kanonik) | `quotations` | `/sd/sales-quotes` | smoke | REAL | REAL |
| SD invoices/forecast/analytics/commission | CRMRoutes:69-72 → **hammasi SDSalesManagement** | — | 4 url 1 sahifa | smoke | yarim — 4 menyu bitta sahifaga taqaladi | DUPLICATE |
| SD manager-panel/warehouse-rental/advance-control | CRMRoutes:78-80 → **hammasi SDExtended** | sd_order_departments | 3 url 1 sahifa | smoke | yarim | DUPLICATE |
| AI CRM | `/ai/crm` + `/ai-crm` ikkalasi AiCrmPage | crm_* | 2 url 1 sahifa | smoke | REAL ko'rinish | DUPLICATE(url) |
| CRM lead email | FE chaqiruvchi grep bo'sh → `crm-leads.controller.ts:186` `return {...sent:true}` | email-log jadval YO'Q | (orphan) | — | yo'q — DB yozmaydi | GREEN-LIE/ORPHAN |
| Customer 360 | `Customer360Page` (`/sd/customers/:id`,`/crm/customer/:id`,`/360`) | sd_customers JOIN | 3 url 1 sahifa | smoke | REAL | REAL/DUP(url) |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP'da SD = mijoz master + kotirovka→buyurtma→yetkazish→hisob-faktura zanjiri bir oqimda, har biri alohida ekran + status workflow. **Bizda:** mijoz CRUD REAL (SAP-darajaga yaqin, rhf+zod validatsiya), lekin invoices/forecast/analytics/commission/manager-panel kabi 7 ta menyu 2 ta umumiy sahifaga (`SDSalesManagement`, `SDExtended`) taqaladi — ekran bor, lekin alohida funksiya yo'q. **Gap:** kotirovka→buyurtma→hisob-faktura→to'lov to'liq workflow FE'da yagona oqim emas. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim.**

### Muammolar (5 tomon mos kelmagan)
- **Q-43 izi REAL tasdiqlangan:** design POST `/design` Q-43'da fake→real qilingan (`docs/audit/parts/10.md:9`), lekin CRM lead email (`crm-leads.controller.ts:186`) hali `return {...sent:true}` — FE grep bo'sh (orphan fake, ma'lumot yo'qolmaydi hozir).
- **Marshrut-duplikat:** 7 SD menyu 2 sahifaga (`SDSalesManagement`×4, `SDExtended`×3) — foydalanuvchi turli menyudan bir xil ekranga tushadi.
- **DB bo'sh:** crm_deals=0, demak konvertatsiya oqimi jonli sinalmagan (xotira: lead→deal convert 500 tuzatilgan, lekin 0 qator).

### 3-qator xulosa
SD mijoz CRUD jonli, REAL, validatsiyali — bu modul eng ishonchli FE qismi. Ammo SD sotuv menyularining yarmi umumiy sahifalarga taqaladi (DUPLICATE url) va to'liq SAP buyurtma-zanjiri FE'da yagona oqim emas. CRM lead email yagona green-lie, ammo orphan (FE chaqirmaydi).

## [100] FE shared/lib (api/i18n/ZVS)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB | FE | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HTTP klient | `lib/api-request.ts` `apiRequest`/`fetchWithAuth`/`getQueryFn` + 4-envelope unwrap (depth=5) | — | barcha sahifa | `lib/__tests__/` | REAL — yagona, qat'iy | REAL |
| Auth refresh | `lib/auth-refresh.ts` `refreshTokenOnce` (in-flight dedup, 401→retry→/login) | — | global | test | REAL — toza | REAL |
| TanStack query | `lib/queryClient.ts` | — | global | — | REAL | REAL |
| i18n | `lib/i18n/` (index/context/hooks/loader/tLabel/constants) FLAT lookup | — | global | `i18n/__tests__/` | REAL | REAL |
| Lokallar | `locales/{uz,ru,uz-cyr}/` har biri 55 JSON fayl | — | — | — | REAL (3-til) | REAL |
| ZVS helper | `lib/sd-helpers.ts`, ZVS sahifa+repo (`director/zvs`) | zvs | `/hr/zvs` | smoke | REAL | REAL |
| Offline | `lib/erp-offline-db.ts`,`pos-db.ts`,`pos-sync.ts` | IndexedDB | POS | — | REAL | REAL |
| Permissions | `lib/permissions.ts`,`roleRoutes.ts` + `PermissionGuard/RoleGate` | — | global | — | REAL | REAL |
| Error logging | `lib/errorLogger.ts`,`fetchInterceptor.ts` | client_errors | global | — | REAL | REAL |
| Export | `lib/export-helpers.ts`,`export-utils.ts` | — | sahifalar | — | REAL | REAL |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP UI5 = OData model + i18n properties + central message handling. **Bizda:** yagona `apiRequest` (4-envelope unwrap — backend nomuvofiqligini yashiradi), 3-til i18n FLAT, offline IndexedDB, permission guards. **Gap:** 4 xil backend envelope mavjudligi o'zi backend-drift belgisi (klient yamoq qiladi). **SAP-darajasi: ✅ shared qatlam SAP-darajaga yaqin (eng kuchli FE qism).**

### Muammolar
- 4 xil response envelope (`{ok,data}` / double-wrap / `{isSuccess,value}` / `T`) — klient hammasini unwrap qiladi, ammo bu BACKEND drift belgisi (FE yamoq).
- i18n FLAT lookup — nested setDeep skriptlar noto'g'ri (xotira), ammo runtime ishlaydi.
- POS alohida `posReq` qatlami — `apiRequest` bilan birlashtirilmagan (2 klient).

### 3-qator xulosa
Shared/lib qatlam FE'ning eng kuchli, eng professional qismi: yagona qat'iy HTTP klient, auth-refresh dedup, 3-til i18n, offline, permission guard. Yagona arxitektura hidi — 4 xil backend envelope'ni klient yamoq qiladi (backend drift belgisi). SAP-darajaga eng yaqin qatlam.

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| jihat | KOD fayl | dalil | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Asosiy ERP responsive | sahifalar Tailwind `sm:/md:/lg:/grid-cols-` | shadcn responsive default | REAL | REAL |
| Sidebar | `components/ui/sidebar.tsx`+`AppSidebar.tsx` | collapse/mobil | REAL | REAL |
| POS layout | `pos-monitor/layout/PosLayout.tsx` inline `style={{display:flex}}` + `.pos-sidebar mini` | Tailwind responsive YO'Q | YARIM | YARIM |
| POS tema | `pos-monitor/styles/pos-theme.css` 14 `@media` | CSS media-query bor (Tailwind emas) | YARIM | YARIM |
| POS barkod | `PosBarcodeScanner.tsx` | planshet kamera kirim | REAL | REAL |
| Mobil accordion | (xotira: MobileAccordionTree o'chirilgan) | — | — | — |

POS responsive grep (`sm:/md:/lg:` `pos-monitor/pages/`) = **0** — POS Tailwind responsive ishlatmaydi, faqat inline-style + pos-theme.css @media (14 ta).

### SAP-taqqos
SAP Fiori = "responsive by default" (UI5 sap.m mobil-first). EWM mobil RF = maxsus planshet ekran. **Bizda:** asosiy ERP shadcn-Tailwind responsive, lekin POS (aynan planshet uchun mo'ljallangan) inline-style + bespoke @media — Tailwind responsive yo'q. **Gap:** POS planshet ekran-o'lchamlari uchun aniq breakpoint testlanmagan; landscape/touch-target o'lcham aniq emas. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim (ERP responsive, POS bespoke).**

### Muammolar
- POS sahifalari `sm:/md:/lg:` ishlatmaydi (grep=0) — responsive faqat pos-theme.css 14 @media orqali, sahifa-darajada emas.
- POS inline `style={{}}` (PosLayout:121-233) — responsive sinash qiyin, breakpoint sahifada yo'q.
- Touch-target o'lcham standartlashtirilmagan (44px SAP/Apple tavsiya).

### 3-qator xulosa
Asosiy ERP shadcn-Tailwind responsive (sm/md/lg) bilan ishlaydi — yaxshi. Ammo POS (aynan planshet uchun) inline-style + bespoke @media ishlatadi, sahifa-darajada Tailwind responsive yo'q (grep=0). Planshet uchun mo'ljallangan modul aslida eng kam responsive-standartlashtirilgan — bu ziddiyat.

## [104] DIZAYN forma validatsiya UX

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| jihat | KOD fayl | dalil | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| rhf+zod forma | 52 sahifa `zodResolver` ishlatadi (grep) | `SDCustomers.tsx:73` zod schema + xato xabari | REAL — inline validatsiya | REAL |
| react-hook-form | 91 sahifa `useForm`/`react-hook-form` | keng tarqalgan | REAL | REAL |
| **Raw useState forma** | **57 sahifa** `useState`-asosli forma (zod YO'Q) | validatsiyasiz | YARIM | YARIM |
| Form primitivi | `ui/form.tsx` FormMessage (xato matni) | shadcn + rhf | REAL | REAL |
| Xato xabar tili | zod xabarlari o'zbek ("Nom kerak") | `SDCustomers.tsx:73` | REAL | REAL |
| Toast feedback | `use-toast` mutation onSuccess/onError | har CRUD | REAL | REAL |

52 sahifa zod (validatsiyali), 91 rhf, lekin 57 sahifa raw useState (validatsiya yo'q yoki qo'lda).

### SAP-taqqos
SAP = field-level validation + message manager (qizil border + tooltip). Odoo = model constraint server-side. **Bizda:** 52 sahifa zod inline-validatsiya + FormMessage + o'zbek xato matni (yaxshi UX), lekin 57 sahifa raw useState (validatsiya nomuvofiq yoki yo'q). **Gap:** validatsiya izchil emas — ~40% formalar zod-himoyasiz. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim (zod-formalar SAP-darajada, raw-formalar past).**

### Muammolar
- **57 sahifa raw useState forma** — zod schema yo'q, validatsiya qo'lda yoki yo'q. Foydalanuvchi noto'g'ri kiritsa, backend 400 qaytarguncha xato ko'rinmaydi.
- Validatsiya izchilligi yo'q — bir sahifa zod, qo'shnisi useState.
- Ba'zi green-lie formalar (QC/Finance approve) validatsiya o'tadi lekin DB yozmaydi (validatsiya UX yaxshi, natija aldov).

### 3-qator xulosa
Forma validatsiya UX bo'lingan: 52 sahifa rhf+zod bilan inline-validatsiya + o'zbek xato matni + FormMessage (SAP-darajaga yaqin), ammo 57 sahifa raw useState (validatsiyasiz). ~40% formalar zod-himoyasiz — bu izchilsizlik eng katta validatsiya-qarz. Toast feedback hamma joyda yaxshi.

## YAKUNIY XULOSA (15 punkt)

**Kuchli tomonlar:** shared/lib (HTTP/i18n/auth) eng professional qatlam; shadcn baza izchil; HR/SD/POS modullari real CRUD (mock deyarli yo'q — 845 sahifada 1 mock massiv); 211 smoke test.

**Asosiy zaifliklar:** (1) **Jadval grid** — 175 sahifaning 95% saralasiz/paginatsiyasiz, `DataTableRedesign` orphan. (2) **GREEN-LIE tasdiqlash** — QC + Finance approve (8 echo endpoint) jonli sahifalardan chaqiriladi, DB yozmaydi (halol deferred). (3) **Davomat green-lie** — route to'qnashuvi. (4) **Forma validatsiya bo'lingan** — 57 sahifa zodsiz. (5) **3-avlod UI** + POS alohida tema — izchillik buzilgan. (6) **URL-duplikat** — SD/SaaS menyular umumiy sahifalarga taqaladi.

**Eng muhim 1 jumla:** FE backbone (api/i18n/auth/shadcn) SAP-darajaga yaqin, lekin grid-funksionallik (sort/filter/pagination) deyarli yo'q va QC/Finance tasdiqlash green-lie — bu ikki nuqta professional ERP hissini eng ko'p pasaytiradi.

## [109] FE error handling (boundary/API xato)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ErrorBoundary (custom) | `components/error-boundary.tsx:50` class + `getDerivedStateFromError` + `componentDidCatch`→`logClientError` | `client_errors` jadval (writer bor) | App.tsx:135, AppRouter har route, ModuleGroup.tsx:31 | `data-testid="error-boundary"` bor | sahifa qulashida fallback | **REAL** |
| Sentry boundary | `main.tsx:95` `<Sentry.ErrorBoundary>` (tashqi qatlam) | Sentry tashqi | root | — | telemetriya | **REAL** |
| Fallback UI i18n | `error-boundary.tsx:20` `useTranslation("common")` `t("errorOccurred")`, `t("refresh")` | — | barcha boundary | — | tarjimalangan xato ekrani | **REAL** |
| API xato (HttpError) | `lib/api-request.ts` `HttpError`, `EPErrorState onRetry` patterni | — | sahifa-darajasi | — | qayta-urinish tugmasi | **REAL** |
| Client error log | `lib/errorLogger.ts` `logClientError` → backend | `client_errors` cap-langan (security audit) | — | — | xatolar yig'iladi | **REAL** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP: markazlashgan message-handling (MessageManager) + tranzaksiya xato qaytishi. Bizda 3-qatlamli himoya (Sentry → custom ErrorBoundary → sahifa EPErrorState) + tarjimalangan fallback + backend log. **Gap:** ba'zi sahifalar `EPErrorState` ishlatmaydi (qo'lda `if(error)`). **SAP-darajasi: ✅ SAP-darajasi** (boundary qatlami mustahkam).

### Muammolar
- Faqat 5 ta TSX `ErrorBoundary`/`componentDidCatch` ishlatadi — lekin App+AppRouter+ModuleGroup mount qilgani uchun qamrov keng (har route o'ralgan).
- Sahifa-darajasi xato-holatlari izchil emas (ba'zilari oddiy matn).

### 3-qator xulosa
Xato-boshqaruv real va ko'p qatlamli: Sentry + custom ErrorBoundary (App/route/module darajasida) + backend `client_errors` log. Fallback UI tarjimalangan. Ba'zi sahifalarda izchil `EPErrorState` yo'q — kichik gap.

| UZ asosiy til | 56 json, ~15147 kalit (DEFAULT_LANGUAGE) | — | barcha | `completeness.test.ts` | to'liq baza | **REAL** |
| UZ qamrov | UZ = baza, hamma kalit mavjud (boshqalar UZ ga fallback) | — | — | — | 100% (ta'rifan baza) | **REAL** |
| "Prettify-key" leak | `common.json`: `bankName="Bank name"`, `fullName="Full name"`, `dueDate="Due date"`, `hireDate="Hire date"`, `btnSubmit="Btn submit"` — ~38 ta auto-gen kalit-prettify | — | — | — | tarjima EMAS, inglizcha | **FAKE-DATA (auto-gen)** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP baza-tili to'liq bo'lishi shart. Bizda UZ = baza, hamma kalit bor. ### 3-qator xulosa
UZ-lotin baza sifatida ta'rifan to'liq (~15147 kalit, barcha fallback shu yerga keladi). Ammo `common.json` ichida ~38 ta auto-generatsiya qilingan "kalit-prettify" inglizcha qoldiq bor (`fullName="Full name"`). Bular tozalanishi kerak.

| Ortiqcha (drift) | **1211 EXTRA kalit** (1059 `common.json`, 64 `warehouse`, 42 `hr`, 30 `kanban`) — UZ da yo'q | — | — | — | drift, ishlatilmaydi | **ORPHAN (extra)** |
| Translit qoldiq | 388 / 16263 qiymat hali LOTIN (2.4%) — aksariyati brend (Telegram, API, EuroPrint) | — | — | — | brend nomlari atayin | **REAL (asosan brend)** |

### Muammolar
- Sidebar `constants.ts` 410 `title:` = eng katta qattiq-matn manbai (navigatsiya til almashmaydi — punkt 106).
- 252 TSX (19%) `t()` ishlatmaydi — ba'zilari faqat-vizual, lekin matnli bo'lsa qattiq.
- HR-pages cluster (Discipline/HRPip/HRMilestones) JSX-text qoldiq.
- `eslint` da `i18n-literal` qoidasi yo'q → yangi qattiq matn aniqlanmaydi.

### 3-qator xulosa
TSX'ning 81% i18n'lik — yaxshi asos. Ammo 19% (252 fayl) tarjimasiz + sidebar 410 qattiq sarlavha + HR-sahifa qoldiqlari qattiq o'zbekcha. Lint-qoidasi yo'qligi yangi qattiq matnning kirib kelishiga yo'l qo'yadi.

## [117] i18n-leak (ingliz FE da)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UZ common.json leak | ~38 kalit qiymati inglizcha kalit-prettify (`bankName="Bank name"`, `dueDate="Due date"`, `fullName="Full name"`, `expiryDate="Expiry date"`) | — | bu kalitни ishlatuvchi sahifa | — | o'zbek inglizcha ko'radi | **FAKE-DATA** |
| RU leak (haqiqiy) | 671 kirill-siz, ~o'nlab haqiqiy (qolgani brend) | — | — | — | rus inglizcha ko'radi | **REAL leak (oz)** |
| uz-cyr leak | 388 lotin qoldiq, aksariyati brend | — | — | — | krill lotin ko'radi | **REAL (asosan brend)** |
| Brend istisnolari | Telegram/API/EuroPrint/Instagram/ZPL — atayin tarjimasiz | — | — | — | normal | **REAL (to'g'ri)** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP: leak = build-fail. Bizda asosiy leak `common.json` dagi ~38 auto-gen inglizcha (`Status absent`, `Marital status`) — bular o'zbek tilida ham inglizcha ko'rinadi. **Gap:** ~38 UZ-leak haqiqiy muammo; RU/krill leak asosan brend. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim.**

### Muammolar
- Eng yomon: UZ `common.json` da ~38 inglizcha qiymat — o'zbek rejimда ham inglizcha (auto-gen artefakt).
- `statusApprovedCompleted="Status: APPROVED → COMPLETED"`, `createShiftCalendar="Create shift calendar"` — sof inglizcha.
- RU/krill da ~o'nlab haqiqiy leak, lekin brend nomlaridan ajratish kerak (statik analiz noto'g'ri-pozitiv beradi).

### 3-qator xulosa
Eng jiddiy leak — UZ `common.json` dagi ~38 auto-generatsiya inglizcha kalit (`Bank name`, `Due date`), bular o'zbek foydalanuvchiga ham inglizcha ko'rinadi. RU/krill leak asosan legitim brend nomlari. Leak-detektor CI yo'q.
