## [118] terminologiya izchilligi

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UZ atama izchilligi | `common.json`: "mijoz"=66, "buyurtma"=149, "klient"=0, "zakaz"=0 | — | — | — | izchil o'zbekcha | **REAL** |
| Sidebar atama | qattiq o'zbekcha, ammo izchil ("Buyurtmalar", "Mijozlar", "To'lovlar") | — | sidebar | — | izchil-u tarjimasiz | **GREEN-LIE** |
| Namespace dublikat | `qc`=`qcreview` (alias), 2 ns bir json | `loader.ts` | QC | — | atama ikkilanishi | **DUPLICATE** |
| POS terminologiya | alohida `pos-monitor/i18n` — ERP common dan ajralgan | — | POS | — | atama bo'linishi | **DUPLICATE** |
| Kross-modul atama | "Avans" (SD) vs umumiy "to'lov" — modul-ichi izchil | — | — | — | — | **REAL** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP: markazlashgan terminologiya-lug'ati (SAPterm) — global izchillik. Bizda UZ atamalari modul-ichi izchil (mijoz/buyurtma standart, klient/zakaz aralashmaydi). LEKIN: ERP i18n ╳ POS i18n bo'linishi + qc/qcreview alias atama-fragmentatsiyasi. **Gap:** markazlashgan glossariy yo'q, 2 i18n stack. **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim.**

### Muammolar
- DUPLICATE: POS o'z i18n stack'i (`usePosI18n`) — ERP `common` bilan umumiy atama-bazasi yo'q → "kirim/chiqim" kabi atamalar 2 joyда mustaqil.
- `qc`/`qcreview` 2 ns bir json — atama-ns ikkilanishi.
- Markazlashgan glossariy/terminologiya hujjati yo'q (faqat `_RU_UNTRANSLATED_AUDIT.md`).

### 3-qator xulosa
UZ ichida atamalar izchil (mijoz/buyurtma standart, sinonim aralashmaydi) — bu yaxshi. Asosiy muammo strukturaviy: ERP ╳ POS 2 i18n stack + qc/qcreview alias atama-fragmentatsiyaga olib keladi. Markazlashgan glossariy yo'q.

## [119] sana/valyuta/raqam format

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| formatDate | `lib/format.ts:formatDate` `toLocaleDateString("uz-UZ")` — HAR DOIM uz-UZ | — | barcha jadval | — | dd.mm.yyyy | **REAL (lekin lokale qotgan)** |
| formatCurrency | `format.ts:formatCurrency` `Intl.NumberFormat("uz-UZ",{currency:"UZS",max/minFractionDigits:0})` | — | moliya | — | UZS 0-kasr | **REAL** |
| formatNumber | `format.ts:formatNumber` `"uz-UZ"` | — | barcha | — | ming-ajratgich | **REAL** |
| fmtMoney (compact) | `format.ts:fmtMoney` "1.5M UZS" K/M/B | — | dashboard | — | ixcham | **REAL** |
| Til-asosli format | YO'Q — `format.ts:15-17` izoh: "RU-locale branch DELIBERATELY NOT included yet" | — | — | — | RU/krill ham uz-UZ format | **GREEN-LIE (single-locale)** |
| null-safe | har formatter `-`/`0` qaytaradi | — | — | — | qulamaydi | **REAL** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP: format foydalanuvchi til/region'iga bog'liq (RU → 1 234,56; UZ → 1 234,56). Bizda format MARKAZLASHGAN va null-safe (yaxshi), LEKIN har doim `uz-UZ` locale — til RU/krill bo'lsa ham UZ-format. Biznes qarori: "hamma 22.04.2026 ni bir xil o'qiydi" (izohlangan). **Gap:** RU foydalanuvchi UZ-locale format ko'radi (ataйин, lekin SAP-darajasi emas). **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 yarim** (markazlashgan, lekin lokale-aware emas).

### Muammolar
- `format.ts` har doim `"uz-UZ"` — `formatCurrency/formatDate/formatNumber` til parametri qabul qilmaydi.
- Valyuta UZS ga qattiq (0-kasr) — chet el mijozlari (USD/EUR) uchun `currency` arg bor, lekin `minimumFractionDigits:0` har doim → USD sentlari yo'qoladi.
- `fmtDate`/`fmtMoney` ham `uz-UZ` qotган.

### 3-qator xulosa
Format qatlami markazlashgan, null-safe va izohlangan — texnik jihatdan toza. Ammo locale qattiq `uz-UZ` ga bog'langan: RU/krill foydalanuvchi ham o'zbek formatini ko'radi, USD valyutasi kasr-qismsiz. Bu atayin biznes-qaror, lekin SAP-darajasidagi locale-awareness emas.

## [120] fallback zanjiri

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB qator | FE sahifa | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3-bosqich i18n fallback | `loader.ts:getTranslation` 1)joriy-til 2)fallback-param 3)UZ(DEFAULT) 4)literal-key | — | barcha | `loader.test.ts` | hech qachon qulamaydi | **REAL** |
| Til-tanlash fallback | `utils.ts:getStoredLanguage` unified→legacy(app_language,europrint-lang)→browser→UZ | localStorage | global | `utils.test.ts` | eski kalit migratsiya | **REAL** |
| `t('key','fallback')` | `hooks.ts` legacy pattern — kalit yo'q bo'lsa fallback | — | legacy callsite | `hooks.test.tsx` | qulamaydi | **REAL** |
| Global `t` stop-gap | `context.tsx` `globalThis.t=_globalT` (hook-siz komponentlar uchun) | — | leftover komponent | — | `t is not defined` oldini oladi | **REAL (texnik qarz)** |
| tLabel const-fallback | `tLabel.ts` const-level (hook chaqirib bo'lmaydigan joy) → fallback UZ | — | const data | — | sidebar/enum | **REAL (reaktiv emas)** |
| safeArray fallback | `queryClient.ts:safeArray` data→items→results→...→[] | — | barcha ro'yxat | — | `.map` xavfsiz | **REAL** |
| api on401 fallback | `queryClient.ts` `getQueryFn({on401:'returnNull'})` | — | public/login | — | sessiyasiz null | **REAL** |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP: til-fallback (RU→EN→raw) + null-handling. Bizda i18n 4-bosqich + til-tanlash 4-bosqich + safeArray envelope-fallback + on401 + global-t stop-gap — juda mustahkam zanjir. **Gap:** `tLabel`/global-t reaktiv emas (til mid-sessiyada o'zgarsa const yangilanmaydi) — bu sidebar qattiq-matnining sababi. **SAP-darajasi: ✅ SAP-darajasi** (fallback eng kuchli punktlardan).

### Muammolar
- `tLabel.ts` va `context.tsx` global-`t` — modul-yuklanganда baholanadi, til mid-sessiyada o'zgarsa REAKTIV emas (izohlangan stop-gap). Bu sidebar/enum til almashmasligining ildiz-sababi.
- Global `t` (`globalThis.t`) — auto-refaktor qoldig'i (har komponentда o'z hook'i bo'lishi kerak edi).

### 3-qator xulosa
Fallback zanjiri eng pishiq qatlamlardan: i18n 4-bosqich + til-tanlash 4-bosqich + safeArray + on401 + global-t himoyasi — hech narsa qulamaydi. Yagona zaiflik — `tLabel`/global-t REAKTIV emas (const-level), bu sidebar va enum-label'larning til-almashmasligini keltirib chiqaradi.

## UMUMIY XULOSA (106–120)

**Kuchli (✅ SAP-darajasi):** FE state (108, TanStack Query 569 fayl), error handling (109, 3-qatlam boundary), i18n yadrosi (111, 3 til/56 ns/static-loader), RU to'liqligi (114, 0 extra), missing-key boshqaruvi (115), fallback zanjiri (120).

**Yarim (🟡):** navigatsiya (106 — struktura SAP-darajasi LEKIN i18n qatlami buzuq), dashboard (107), UZ-lotin (112 — ~38 leak), uz-cyr (113 — 1211 drift), hardcoded (116 — sidebar+19% TSX), i18n-leak (117), terminologiya (118 — 2 stack), format (119 — locale qotgan).

**Yo'q (🔴):** accessibility (110 — aria 4.3%, WCAG yo'q).

**Eng muhim 5 muammo:**
1. **Sidebar i18n buzuq** (106/116/118): jonli ERP sidebar (`ModuleSidebar`→`navigationGroups`) 410 qattiq o'zbekcha `title:`, `titleKey:`=0 → til almashtirilganда menyu o'zgarmaydi. 2 parallel sidebar (HR-only i18n'lik ╳ ERP hardcoded).
2. **a11y deyarli yo'q** (110): aria-* 4.3%, WCAG/axe test 0, high-contrast yo'q.
3. **UZ common.json ~38 inglizcha leak** (112/117): `fullName="Full name"`, `dueDate="Due date"` auto-gen — o'zbek ham inglizcha ko'radi.
4. **uz-cyr key-drift** (113): 1211 ortiqcha + 95 yetishmagan; CI parity'ni majburlamaydi.
5. **Format locale qotgan** (119): har doim `uz-UZ`, RU/krill ham UZ-format; USD kasr yo'qoladi.

> - BE "e2e" spec: `test/e2e/*.e2e-spec.ts` = **17** (HAMMASI `jest.fn()` mock, real DB YO'Q).
> - FE Vitest test: **401** (`.smoke.test.tsx` = **262**; `pages/` 296 dan 279 tasi faqat render).
> - Playwright spec: **49** (CIda faqat **1** — `aisha-director-flow`, mock-backend; 34 tasi `toContain(res.status())` status-soak; 21 tasi `expect(true).toBe(true)`).
> - CI: **2** workflow (`ci.yml`, `code-quality.yml`).

## [136] TEST stack (Jest/Vitest/Playwright)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB | FE/config | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| BE Jest | `apps/api/test/jest.config.js` (testRegex `test/.*\.spec\.ts$`) | — | `apps/api/package.json` `test`/`test:ci`/`test:dto`/`test:e2e`/`test:mutation` | 681 spec | Real unit runner | REAL |
| FE Vitest | `artifacts/erp-dashboard/vitest.config.ts` (jsdom, v8 coverage, setup `src/test/setup.ts`) | — | `package.json` `test`/`test:coverage` | 401 test | Real component runner | REAL |
| Playwright | `artifacts/erp-dashboard/playwright.config.ts` (chromium, mock-backend webServer) | — | `test:e2e`/`test:e2e:api`/`test:e2e:ui` | 49 spec | Brauzer E2E (lekin CIda 1) | REAL (cheklangan) |
| Stryker (mutation) | `apps/api/stryker.config.json` (jest runner, mutate finance/hr/sd) | — | `test:mutation` | — | Mutation testing | ORPHAN (CI da chaqirilmaydi) |
| Testcontainers | `apps/api/test/_setup/test-db.ts` (`PostgreSqlContainer`) | — | `describeWithDb` Docker yo'q bo'lsa `describe.skip` | 1 helper | Real DB integ. uchun infra | ORPHAN (hech kim chaqirmaydi) |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo**: Odooda Python `TransactionCase` real DB transaksiya bilan; SAP ABAP Unit + ATC. Stack mavjudligi standart.
- **Bizda**: 3 ta industriya-standart runner (Jest+Vitest+Playwright) to'liq sozlangan. Stack TANLOVI SAP darajasida.
- **Gap**: testcontainers helperi yozilgan-u, hech bir spec uni ishlatmaydi — real-DB integ. qatlami amalda yo'q.
- **SAP-daraja**: 🟡 (stack ✅, lekin integ-runner ishlatilmagan)

### Muammolar
1. `test-db.ts` (testcontainer) — `find` 1 ta fayl, lekin spec'larda `startTestDb()` chaqiruvi 0 ta → infratuzilma o'lik.
2. Stryker o'rnatilgan, `stryker.config.json` to'g'ri, lekin `.github/` ichida `test:mutation` chaqiruvi 0 (faqat qo'lda).
3. 4 xil Jest config (`jest.config`, `jest.e2e.config`, `jest.dto.config`) — dto/e2e default `pnpm test`ga kirmaydi.

### 3-qator xulosa
Test stacki uchta professional runner bilan to'liq o'rnatilgan va sozlangan — bu qism SAP yaqin.
Lekin testcontainers infra o'lik va Stryker CIga ulanmagan: stack bor, intizom yarim.
Verdikt: stack REAL, integ-runner + mutation amalda ishlatilmaydi.

## [137] TEST coverage haqiqiy (BE/FE %)

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB | FE | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| BE threshold | `jest.config.js` `coverageThreshold global` lines/stmts **25**, func 25, branch 20 | — | — | gate bor | Past, lekin gate REAL | REAL (past bar) |
| FE threshold | `vitest.config.ts` `thresholds` lines/stmts **15**, func 15, branch **10** | — | — | gate bor | "Intentionally low baseline (5%)" izoh | REAL (juda past bar) |
| BE haqiqiy % | `pnpm test -- --coverage` (CI `code-quality.yml`) → codecov | — | — | o'lchanadi | Codecov uploadi `fail_ci_if_error:false` | GREEN-LIE (CI coverage'da yiqilmaydi) |
| FE haqiqiy % | xuddi shunday, `fail_ci_if_error:false` | — | — | o'lchanadi | Smoke 262 → branch coverage shishadi | GREEN-LIE |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo**: SAP modullari 80%+ kod-coverage talab qiladi (release gate). Odoo core ~70%.
- **Bizda**: deklarativ bar BE 25% / FE **10–15%** — SAP'dan 3–5 barobar past. Vitest izohida ochiq tan olingan: *"Intentionally low baseline (5%) so we can ratchet upward"*.
- **Gap**: 262 smoke test "render qildi" deb branch/statement coverage'ni sun'iy ko'taradi → raqam haqiqiy mantiq qamrovini ko'rsatmaydi. Codecov `fail_ci_if_error:false` → coverage CI'ni yiqitmaydi.
- **SAP-daraja**: 🔴

### Muammolar
1. FE branch threshold = 10% — bu coverage emas, formallik. `vitest.config.ts` izohi buni tasdiqlaydi.
2. 262 smoke + 149 stub + 42 tavtologik test coverage raqamini shishiradi (kod yuklanadi, lekin tasdiqlanmaydi).
3. CI'da coverage yiqilmaydi (`fail_ci_if_error:false`, codecov token bilan) → threshold faqat lokal jest gate'da real (CI Vitestda flag yo'q).

### 3-qator xulosa
Coverage o'lchanadi va codecovga yuklanadi, lekin barlar SAP'dan keskin past (BE 25 / FE 10–15).
262 smoke + 149 stub test coverage raqamini sun'iy shishiradi — "yashil" ham ko'p narsani isbotlamaydi.
Verdikt: coverage mexanizmi REAL, ammo raqam GREEN-LIE darajasida ishonchsiz.

| FE smoke | `artifacts/erp-dashboard/src/**/*.smoke.test.tsx` = **262** | — | har sahifa | "renders without throwing" (`container.firstChild not.toBeNull`) | Render crashini ushlaydi | REAL (sayoz) |
| BE tavtologik | 42 spec `from src` import qilmaydi (mas. `qc/spc-fmea.spec.ts`) | — | — | local reimpl test | O'z nusxasini test qiladi | FAKE-DATA |

**Dalil (stub):** `_stubs/AdminExtraService.spec.ts` — `it('happy: module exports the class')` faqat `expect(mod.AdminExtraService ?? mod.default).toBeDefined()`. Hech bir metod chaqirilmaydi.
**Dalil (smoke):** `pages/ErpRoadmapCard.smoke.test.tsx` — `render(<Page/>); expect(container.firstChild).not.toBeNull()`. Tugma/forma/state tekshirilmaydi.
**Dalil (tavtologik):** `qc/spc-fmea.spec.ts` ichida `mean`/`stdDev`/`buildControlChart` LOKAL yozilgan; `grep "from src" = 0` → production SPC servisini test QILMAYDI.

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo**: "import ishladi" testi qabul qilinmaydi — har test biznes-behavior tasdiqlashi shart.
- **Bizda**: 681 BE spec'ning 149 (22%) + FE 296 page'ning 279 (94%) faqat sayoz. Audit'ning "149 stub + 262 smoke" da'vosi **AYNAN TASDIQLANDI**.
- **Gap**: ~191/681 BE test (149 stub + 42 tavtologik = 28%) hech qanday biznes-mantiqni tasdiqlamaydi.
- **SAP-daraja**: 🔴

### Muammolar
1. 149 stub — class konstruktor ekanini tekshiradi, davranishni emas. 100% "yashil" lekin 0 qiymat.
2. 262 smoke — faqat "crash bo'lmadi"; bo'sh sahifa ham o'tadi (GREEN-LIE'ga yaqin foydaviy ma'noda).
3. 42 tavtologik BE test (`spc-fmea`, root `qc-spc-fmea` 9 import lekin lokal funksiya) — production kodga ulanmagan.

### 3-qator xulosa
Audit raqamlari aniq: 149 stub (`_stubs/`) + 262 smoke (`.smoke.test.tsx`) jonli skanda tasdiqlandi.
Bu fayllar test sonini shishiradi-yu (1000+ test) biznes-mantiq qamrovini deyarli oshirmaydi.
Verdikt: 149 stub = ORPHAN/FAKE, 262 smoke = sayoz-REAL, 42 = tavtologik FAKE.

### 5-tomonlama jadval
| feature | KOD fayl:satr | DB | FE | TEST | FOYDA | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ci.yml | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (typecheck job) | — | — | typecheck | TS gate | REAL |
| code-quality.yml | `.github/workflows/code-quality.yml` 6 job | PG15+Redis service | Vitest | test-backend/frontend/architecture/e2e/i18n/security | To'liq CI | REAL |
| BE test CI | `code-quality.yml:54` `pnpm test -- --coverage` + `db push` real schema | europrint_test | — | jest+coverage | Real DB test | REAL |
| E2E CI | `code-quality.yml` `test-e2e` faqat `if: pull_request` | — | playwright | 1 spec (testMatch) | — | GREEN-LIE (faqat 1 mock-test) |
| Stryker CI | YO'Q | — | — | — | — | ORPHAN |
| coverage gate | `fail_ci_if_error:false` | — | — | — | — | GREEN-LIE |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo**: CI har commitda to'liq test + coverage gate (yiqitadi).
- **Bizda**: audit "2 workflow" — tasdiqlandi (`ci.yml`+`code-quality.yml`). `code-quality.yml` jiddiy: real Postgres15+Redis service, `db push` bilan schema, BE+FE coverage, 22 arxitektura skripti, Semgrep, i18n-leak gate. Bu SAP-yaqin INFRA.
- **Gap**: (a) Playwright CI'da `if: pull_request` + `testMatch=['aisha-director-flow']` → faqat 1 mock E2E. (b) codecov `fail_ci_if_error:false` → coverage CI'ni yiqitmaydi. (c) Stryker hech qachon CI'da. (d) FE Vitest CI'da threshold flag yo'q.
- **SAP-daraja**: 🟡 (infra kuchli, gate'lar yumshoq)

### Muammolar
1. `playwright.config.ts:13` `CI_TEST_MATCH=['aisha-director-flow.spec.ts']` → 49 E2E'dan CI'da faqat 1 ishlaydi (mock-backend).
2. `code-quality.yml` codecov `fail_ci_if_error:false` ikki joyda → coverage past bo'lsa ham PR o'tadi.
3. Stryker (`test:mutation`) hech bir workflowda chaqirilmaydi — mutation gate yo'q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Audit "2 workflow" aniq; `code-quality.yml` real PG+Redis service bilan jiddiy 6-jobli CI — infra SAP-yaqin.
Lekin gate'lar yumshoq: E2E CI'da 1 ta mock-test, coverage yiqitmaydi, Stryker umuman yo'q.
Verdikt: CI strukturasi REAL, gating kuchi GREEN-LIE darajasida yumshoq.

- **SAP-daraja**: 🟡 (PP/MES ✅, QC-SPC FAKE, MES→QC bog'lanish testsiz)

### Muammolar
1. `qc/spc-fmea.spec.ts` + `qc-spc-fmea.spec.ts` — lokal `mean/stdDev/buildControlChart`, production SPC servisni test qilmaydi (soxta coverage).

### 3-qator xulosa

| POS manfiy stok | `pos-balance-guard.service.spec.ts` (db mock) | warehouse_stock=25 | — | unit | Stok himoya | REAL (db-mock) |
| GL real posting | jonli `entries` jadvaliga real INSERT E2E | **entries=0** | — | YO'Q | — | ORPHAN |
| Pul→GL→ledger E2E | MEMORY: "POS→GL→log not ledger" uzilishi | — | — | regress yo'q | — | ORPHAN |

Lekin eng kritik joy — real DB ga yozilish, GL ledger izchilligi, ikki-stok-jadval ziddiyati — db mock sabab HIMOYASIZ.

- CODEOWNERS guard (`check-codeowners.mjs`) kritik fayllarni himoya qiladi — bu add-A muammosini qisman yumshatadi (kritik fayl tasodifan staged bo'lsa blok).

### 3-qator xulosa
Commit governance qisman REAL: format gate + anti-revert + CODEOWNERS jonli ishlaydi.
"No git add -A / individual commit" qoidasi faqat hujjatda — avtomat enforce yo'q (inson-ishonchi).
SAP-darajasi: ✅ format/owner uchun, 🟡 add-A enforce uchun.

### 5-tomonlama jadval

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| lint-staged (package.json) | `package.json:152-162` | — | — | pre-commit | eslint+prettier staged | REAL |
| `.lintstagedrc.json` | `.lintstagedrc.json` "passive" izoh | — | — | — | Eskirgan/yanglish | ORPHAN (stale) |
| Prettier config ×2 | `.prettierrc` (singleQuote:false,100) ╳ `.prettierrc.json` (singleQuote:true,120) | — | — | — | Format | DUPLICATE (ziddiyat) |
| ESLint max-warnings gate | `ci.yml` `eslint --max-warnings 0` | — | — | CI | BE lint gate | REAL |

### SAP-taqqos
SAP'da: bitta `.prettierrc` + ESLint config, CI gate. | Bizda: lint-staged + ESLint CI gate REAL, lekin 2 ta ziddiyatli Prettier config + eskirgan `.lintstagedrc.json`. | Gap: prettier sozlamasi 2 xil (qaysi biri yutadi noaniq) → izchillik buzilishi. | SAP-darajasi: 🟡

- `.prettierrc` (`singleQuote:false`, printWidth 100) ╳ `.prettierrc.json` (`singleQuote:true`, printWidth 120) — IKKI ZIDDIYATLI config. Prettier qaysi birini oladi loyiha tuzilishiga bog'liq → format izchilligi xavf ostida.

### 3-qator xulosa
Lint/format gate ISHLAYDI (lint-staged + BE ESLint CI), lekin config izchilligi buzuq.

| V2 Kassir = naqd-nazorat markazi | YO'Q — `pos/.../cash-register.repository.ts:30` `insert(retail_pos_transactions)` = **chakana POS** (savat/QQS/qaytim) | `retail_pos_transactions`=0, `cash_sessions`=0, `cash_registers`=0 | "Kassa" (retail) | yo'q | Noto'g'ri konsept; naqd-hub yo'q | GREEN-LIE/ORPHAN |

| V2 oylik/avans tarqatish → kassir | payroll real (`FinanceExtendedPayrollService`), avans `check-advance.handler` real, **kassirga ulanmagan** | 0 shartnoma | avans FE yo'q | yo'q | Dvigatel bor, hub yo'q | ORPHAN |
| V3 13 ombor turi (config-driven) | `warehouse_types` config-driven | 9 tur jonli (13/9) | `warehouse/hub` Tabs | yo'q | Tur filtri ishlaydi | REAL (qisman) |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo'da:** POS = real kassa (smena, X/Z hisobot, naqd sanash, kassir PIN, kunlik yopilish). Ombor = MM/WM (qabul→QC→put-away→bin, batch/serial, transfer order).

Ammo "Kassa" = chakana do'kon POS (noto'g'ri konsept); naqd-nazorat hub, smena, kassir PIN yo'q. Ombor turi 9/13 + Excel ko'rinish bor, lekin Karantin/QC qabul bosqichi (SAP'ning MM quality inspection) yo'q.
- **Gap:** kassir-hub konsepti, smena/X-Z hisobot, qabul QC; **SAP-darajasi: 🟡 (POS-ombor REAL ~65%, kassir 🔴 ~15%).**

### Muammolar (5-tomon mos kelmaslik)

POS Monitor (zavod ombori) va Ombor (9 tur + Excel) REAL va stokni atomik o'zgartiradi — eng kuchli bo'g'inlardan. Kassir esa noto'g'ri konsept (retail POS), 0 yozuvchi, hub UI yo'q. SAP-darajasi: ombor/POS 🟡 yetuk, kassir 🔴 deyarli yo'q.

Ammo (a) `manager_id` 0/30 NULL → reports-to bo'sh → tasdiq zanjiri uziladi; (b) lavozim→kerakli jihozlar modeli (`position_equipment`) yo'q; (c) getNodeHistory 501.
- **Gap:** manager_id/reports-to to'ldirish, position requirements (equipment); **SAP-darajasi: 🟢/🟡 (org-tree+RBAC REAL ~75%, lekin reports-to data-gap).**

### Muammolar
1. DB: `manager_id` 0/30 NULL, head 18/142 → reports-to skeleti to'ldirilmagan (tasdiq/eskalatsiya uziladi).
2. KOD: `position_equipment` jadval va equipment ustuni yo'q → "kerakli jihozlar" vizyoni yo'q.
3. KOD: getNodeHistory 501 → konstruktor tarix qismi yo'q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Org-sxema (142 dept/96 lavozim interaktiv tree + export) va position-RBAC (1380 ruxsat, rostan bloklaydi) — tizimning eng kuchli "tana"si, HAQIQATAN qurilgan. Portret konstruktor wizard real. Ammo `manager_id` 0/30 NULL reports-to'ni o'ldiradi va lavozim→kerakli-jihozlar modeli yo'q. SAP-darajasi 🟢/🟡 ~75%.

## [174] Vizyon 21 GOLDEN THREAD (uchma-uch, qayerda uziladi)

### 5-tomonlama jadval (zanjir bo'g'inlari — `TRANSMISSIYA-XARITA` §4A qayta isbotlandi)
| bo'g'in | KOD fayl:satr | DB dalil | holat | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CRM lead→deal→SD order | `mark-deal-won.handler.ts:47` → `deal-won.listener.ts:15` → `create-order.handler` real INSERT | `crm_deals`=0, sales_orders 12/12 crm_deal_id NULL | kod ulangan, jonli 0 | REAL/qisman |
| SD order INSERT | `queries-sd.ts` `.insert(sd_sales_orders)` (VIEW→sales_orders, insertable) | `sales_orders`=12 | haqiqiy INSERT | REAL |
| **SD order → Production** | **FK=0** (`information_schema`); `pp.module.ts` da `OrderCreatedEvent` listener YO'Q | orders↔sales_orders FK 0 ta | ⛔ UZILGAN (backbone 1-bo'g'in) | YO'Q |
| Production → MES routing | `PpReleasedEvent` → faqat MM listener (MES emas); MES qo'lda POST | `pp_routing_operations`=0 | UZUQ | YO'Q |
| MES → QC | `complete-session.handler.ts:71` → `mes-completed.listener.ts:28-39` **no-op stub** | `qc_inspections`=0 | UZUQ (handler yo'q) | UZUQ |
| QC → Ombor | `qc-passed.listener.ts:20` → `receiveFg()` → `drizzle-wms.repo.ts:51` insert **`stocks`** | `stocks`=0 (≠warehouse_stock 25) | QISMAN (noto'g'ri jadval) | GREEN-LIE |
| Ombor → SD yetkazish | `wms-fg-received.listener.ts:25-29` orderId undefined → **skip** | `ow_deliveries`=0 | UZUQ | UZUQ |
| Avans → CC fan-out (5 bo'lim) | `advance-approved-fanout.listener.ts:25-87` real idempotent INSERT | `sd_order_departments`=0 | ✅ ULANGAN (kod) | REAL (bo'sh) |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** Order-to-Cash (SD→PP→MM→FI) va Procure-to-Pay zanjirlari avtomatik hujjat oqimi bilan (sales order → production order → goods movement → billing → accounting) — bitta document flow.
- **Bizda golden thread:** **birinchi bo'g'inda (SD→Production) UZILADI** — `orders` ╳ `sales_orders` orasida FK yo'q, PP'da `OrderCreatedEvent` listener yo'q. Shu sababli undan keyingi hamma narsa (MES, QC, Ombor, Yetkazish) jonli hech qachon ishlamaydi. Yagona REAL bo'g'in = avans fan-out (5 bo'lim).
- **Gap:** SD→Production ko'prik (kanonik buyurtma + FK + listener); **SAP-darajasi: 🔴 (10 bo'g'indan ~1 ishlaydi).**

### Muammolar
1. KOD: `pp.module.ts` listeneri orasida `OrderCreatedEvent` YO'Q (faqat 5 ta: Advance/MroStop/Design/LabTest/WmsGoodsIssued).
2. DB: `orders`↔`sales_orders` FK = 0 ta (ikki buyurtma dunyosi); `orders` jadval umuman DROP qilingan (2026-06-06).
3. KOD: `mes-completed.listener.ts:28-39` no-op stub; `qc-passed` → `stocks` (noto'g'ri jadval); `wms-fg-received` → skip (orderId yo'q).

### 3-qator xulosa
Golden thread **birinchi bo'g'inda uziladi**: SD order → Production o'tmaydi (FK yo'q + `OrderCreatedEvent` listener yo'q), shuning uchun MES/QC/Ombor/Yetkazish jonli hech qachon ishlamaydi. Yagona REAL modullararo bog'lanish = avans→5 bo'lim fan-out. 10 zanjir bo'g'inidan faqat ~1 ishlaydi. SAP-darajasi 🔴.

## [175] Vizyon 22 integratsiya (modullar ulanganmi)

### 5-tomonlama jadval (event-bus + transmissiya — `TRANSMISSIYA-XARITA` §2,§4D)
| integratsiya | KOD dalil | holat | verdikt |
|---|---|---|---|
| Event-bus (EventEmitter2 + outbox) | `outbox-publisher.service.ts:31` har 10s `domain_events` o'qiydi | `domain_events`=0 → to'g'ridan emit | QISMAN |
| 0-listener eventlar | `DealLostEvent`/`StockUpdatedEvent`/`InvoicePartiallyPaid`/SOS + agent string (`stock.critical`/`iot.anomaly`/`crm.hot_leads_found`) — publisher bor, listener yo'q | 13+ event bo'shlikka chiqadi | ORPHAN |
| Order→Kanban | `order-created-kanban.handler.ts:29` real | kanban_cards=2 | REAL |
| Kanban→CC / Kanban→Kassir | listener YO'Q | — | YO'Q |
| Avans→fan-out | `advance-approved-fanout.listener.ts` real | sd_order_departments=0 | REAL (bo'sh) |
| Pul→GL | faqat `record-payment.handler.ts:91` → entries (qo'lda); MM/invoice/avans/POS/depreciation UZUQ | entries=0 | QISMAN |
| String/class double-emit | `PosMovementCompletedEvent` CLASS + `'pos.movement.data.completed'` STRING → ba'zi listener 2× | — | DUP risk |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP/Odoo'da:** modullar bitta ma'lumotlar bazasi + avtomatik posting/workflow orqali chambarchas ulangan (bitta document flow, real-time). 
- **Bizda:** event-bus infratuzilmasi (EventEmitter2 + outbox relay) MAVJUD va **ULANGAN** (outbox real ishlaydi), lekin (a) `domain_events`=0 → outbox bo'sh; (b) 13+ event 0-listener (bo'shlikka chiqadi); (c) string/class double-emit; (d) asosiy backbone bo'g'inlari uzilgan. Faqat order→kanban va avans→fan-out REAL.
- **Gap:** backbone listenerlari, 0-listener eventlarni ulash, double-emit tozalash; **SAP-darajasi: 🔴 (modullararo integratsiya ~15%).**

### Muammolar
1. KOD: 13+ event publisher'i bor, listener'i yo'q (bo'shlikka chiqadi) — `TRANSMISSIYA-XARITA` §4D.
2. DB: `domain_events`=0 → outbox relay ishlaydi lekin hech narsa o'tkazmaydi.
3. KOD: `PosMovementCompletedEvent` ikki marta (CLASS+STRING) chiqariladi → double-process xavfi.

### 3-qator xulosa
Event-bus infratuzilmasi (EventEmitter2 + outbox relay) mavjud va ULANGAN, lekin amalda modullararo integratsiya ~15% — 13+ event 0-listener, asosiy backbone uzilgan, faqat order→kanban va avans→fan-out REAL. Modullar = "20 orol, ko'prik deyarli yo'q". SAP-darajasi 🔴.

## [176] SAP-GAP umumiy (tizim SAP qaysi %)

### Jamlama jadval (manba: YAKUNIY-XULOSA §1, GAP-1 §147)
| O'lcham | % | Dalil |
|---|---|---|
| Backend (ichki qurilish) | ~85% | DDD/CQRS/Result/4 global guard real; xavfsizlik HAQIQIY |
| Ekranlar (har modul alohida) | ~57% | 132 FE sahifa; ko'pi ko'rinishda tayyor |
| **Modullar bog'lanishi** | **~15%** | 10 backbone bog'lanishdan ~1 ishlaydi |
| Operatsion data | ~0-2% | Deyarli hamma jadval 0 qator (qurilish bosqichi) |
| **Egasi vizyoni (SAP-darajali aqlli zavod)** | **~30%** | "Tana bor, asab/qon ulanmagan" |

### SAP-taqqos
- **SAP'da:** to'liq integratsiyalangan, real-time, document-flow, avtomatik posting, multi-org. 100% = SAP/Odoo professional.
- **Bizda:** **poydevor sifatida ~85%** (real, xavfsiz, professional kod — bu nodir), lekin **ishlaydigan biznes-tizim sifatida ~15%** (ulanish + data + o'zak yetishmaydi). SAP'gacha bo'lgan masofa: integratsiya qatlami + master data + ikki-dunyo birlashtirish + GL avtoposting.
- **Umumiy SAP-GAP: tizim SAP funksionalligining ~25-30% darajasida** (skelet boy, jonli oqim past).

### Muammolar
1. "%" raqamlari ALDAYDI: modul 70% bo'lsa-da biznes-jarayonga hissasi ~10% (jarayon ulanish joyida uziladi).
2. Backend 85% ╳ integratsiya 15% — bu farq = SAP-gap yadrosi.
3. Data 0 → SAP "ishlaydi" deganda data + jonli oqim nazarda tutiladi, bu hali yo'q.

### 3-qator xulosa
Tizim SAP funksionalligining **~25-30%** darajasida: poydevor (backend) ~85% professional va real, lekin ishlaydigan integratsiyalangan ERP sifatida ~15%. SAP-gap = ulanish qatlami + master data + ikki-dunyo birlashtirish. "Buzuq emas — ulanmagan."

## [180] YAKUNIY halol javob (bugun nima, SAP uchun nima kerak)

### BUGUN NIMA BOR (halol)
- **Poydevor (backend) ~85%** — DDD/CQRS/Result, 4 global guard, RBAC server-enforce (1380 ruxsat), audit 9322 yozuv. Bu **nodir** — ko'p ERP shu yerda yiqiladi, bu yiqilmaydi.
- **REAL ishlaydigan yadrolar:** Ombor/POS Monitor (atomik stok), org-sxema+RBAC (142 dept/96 lavozim), Kanban dvigatel, avans→5 bo'lim fan-out (yagona real modullararo), Chat (jonli, 34 xabar — ishlatilgan), LMS, AI planner algoritmlari, payroll hisoblash, CC backend (AI intervyu/PIN/SLA).
- **132 FE sahifa** — har modul alohida ~57% ko'rinishda tayyor.

### BUGUN NIMA YO'Q (SAP-gacha)
- Modullararo **integratsiya ~15%** (10 backbone bog'lanishdan 1).
- Golden thread **1-bo'g'inda uziladi** (SD→Production).
- **Master data tarqoq** (2 order/2 GL/2 stock/manager_id 0).
- **Operatsion data ~0** (qurilish bosqichi).
- Kassir noto'g'ri konsept; SMM-AI/lead-gen/IoT-tablet stub; kg↔list formula yo'q; OEE fake.

### SAP UCHUN NIMA KERAK (tartib bilan)
1. **O'zak:** manager_id backfill + kanonik order jadval tanlash + GL avtoposting listener.
2. **Bitta oltin ip:** Buyurtma→Avans→IshChiq→Ombor→Yetkazish→Moliya — boshdan-oxir bitta real buyurtma.
3. **Master data birlashtirish:** ikki-dunyo (order/GL/stock/task/lead) → bitta haqiqat manbai.
4. **Stub-to'ldirish:** SMM-AI, IoT-tablet, routing-create, MES→QC, kg↔list formula.
5. **Data + test:** tor bir bo'lakda jonli ishga tushirish + vizyon-modul spec'lar.

### SAP-darajasi (yakuniy): 🟡/🔴
- Poydevor 🟢 (B), ishlaydigan ERP 🔴 (D+), SAP-funksionallik ~25-30%.

### 3-qator xulosa
Bugun: real, xavfsiz, professional poydevor (~85%) + 20 yaxshi ishlangan modul-orol, lekin orollar orasida ko'prik (~15%) va data (~0) yo'q. SAP uchun: o'zak (manager_id/kanonik-order/GL) → bitta oltin ip → master-data birlashtirish → stub-to'ldirish → data+test. Bu "ulamasdan qolganni ulash", qayta-yozish EMAS — qutqarib bo'ladigan, aniq yo'li bor holat.
